The same was true in the corporate structure, where divorced people rarely moved up the executive ladder. Now corporations welcome a divorced man, because they can shift him around the country without worrying about relocating his family or making certain that they are happy.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//11a71f71-ee18-4dc9-92e7-d9c881affe4a/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_191_546_1001_877.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A25Z%2F-1%2F%2F853c7450010d669fb5e16f7d472e9222af718ad62b8daf5fba34f8991525e31f" alt="Image" width="68%" /></div>


口试部分

# 口试全真预测试题(一)及答案

#### 【主题】 $ \underline{\text{University life}} $

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

What rules and regulations are there in your university? Name one or two.

There are many rules concerning all aspects of campus life in my university. These rules are written in a handbook, which is given to every student. For example, students are forbidden to be barebacked on campus. Everybody should be quiet in the classroom and library. Cribbing is strictly prohibited, and the cribber will be punished sternly. If a student cheats in an examination, his or her degree will be cancelled. So, no student dares to cheat in examinations. There are many other regulations in my university that help keep our campus in order.

Note:

barebacked: 赤膊

crib: 抄袭，剽窃

vest: 背心

cribber: 作弊者

##### 技巧点拨：

本题很显然是要考生对学校的管理条例进行举例说明，

考生只需尽量去想像即可。

Do you think rules and regulations in the university are stricter than those in high school? (In what way?)

No, I don't think rules and regulations in the university are stricter than those in high school. On the contrary, I think the rules and regulations are quite lenient. In high school, the only thing we can do is to study, study and study. Many things are forbidden, such as being late for class, dancing and having love affairs among students. Teachers are very strict with students. If you play basketball for over two hours, teachers will criticize you. However in university, you can take part in many kinds of leisure activities which were forbidden in high school.

Teachers will not restrain students. To some extent, the campus life at university chiefly depends on self-discipline.

Note:

lenient: 宽大的，仁慈的

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//11a71f71-ee18-4dc9-92e7-d9c881affe4a/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_119_366_151_397.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A27Z%2F-1%2F%2F03930c7b03985be61ff8d8351910453981a2d7d8fbfbbea7d4a3b1fa5a9a480d" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


Which is more important to good conduct, regulations or self-discipline?

I think self-discipline is more important in comparison with regulations. Regulations are for the sake of better self-discipline. They are the outset of self-discipline, and can train students to be self-regulating. So without regulations, there would be no self-discipline. However, self-discipline is more important. If a student won't obey the rules, regulations would be useless. A well-ordered campus radically depends on self-discipline.

Note:

outset: 开端

radically:从根本上

技巧点拨：

本题实际上是回答管理以及自律两者之间的关系。在回答问题的时候，一定要辩证地看问题，同时指出其中更重要的是哪一个并说明原因，这样的答案才算完整。

What do you think of the rules and regulations in your university?

Generally speaking, the rules and regulations in my university are satisfactory, although some rules are still unreasonable from the point of our students. An example of a satisfactory rule is that students should not make noise in the classroom and library, which makes our classrooms and library quiet, and then students can concentrate themselves on study. The rules against cribbing put an end to such a practice, and no student dares to cheat in examinations. However, we students are still a bit unsatisfied with some rules, because we just think them unreasonable or meaningless. Take the rules of electricity as an example. It's unreasonable to shut off the electricity at 11 o'clock every night. In fact, it's not convenient to our lives especially in the summer. We can't use fans without electricity, so we often wake up because of the heat.

Note:

satisfactory: 令人满意的

put an end to: 结束，终止

##### 技巧点拨：

上面的参考答案先从一般的意义上给出基本满意的答案，之后又提出其中学生存在一定意见的地方，这样的答题方式是值得考生学习的地方。

Have any of your classmates ever broken university regulations? (Please give an example.)

Yes, several of my classmates have broken university regulations, and they were punished rigorously. It was during last summer when we were having our final examinations. My classmates helped their friends who were students from another department of my university by taking their examinations. Finally some students disclosed them, and then they were punished. Their degrees were all cancelled. So now no student dares to crib in examination.

Note:

rigorously: 严厉地

技巧点拨：

注意题目要求是举一个例子，考生一定要听清考官所说的话，不要跑题。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

From my point of view, even though there was a perfect rules system which involved every detailed aspect of campus life, without good regulation, the rules system would be of no use. For example, the teacher of the library should stop the students from making noises; the invigilator should invigilate the students strictly; the conductor of dormitory buildings should inspect the building frequently to avoid the use of electric appliance. As long as they do well in their jobs, there should be no violations of the rules and regulations. Otherwise, students would violate rules and regulations frequently.

But universities don't have enough money to invest in the supervision of rules and regulations. It's really costly.

A Yes, what you said is reasonable. If students can do well in self-discipline, we won't have to worry about the high cost of supervision. So, I think the fact why some of the rules and regulations are violated is due to the neglecting of rules and regulation education.

③ Oh, I couldn't agree more. As everybody knows, prevention is not effective but self-discipline. University should pay attention to the education of rules and regulations, and make the students realize the serious consequences of violating the

rules and regulations. If students can do well in self-discipline, they can also supervise each other.

I think prevention is still effective. As long as universities do well in supervision, it will be OK. You said the supervision is costly. I don't think so. As long as the punishment method is rigorous, the violations can be stopped. So I can't understand why some of the rules and regulations are frequently broken. The reason is that the punishment method is not rigorous enough. For example, if a cribber's degree is cancelled there would be no other student who would still dare to do such a thing.

B Yes, I agree with you. It has been proven by many facts.

From my point of view, there are several reasons why some of the rules and regulations are frequently broken, such as bad regulations, no rigorous punishment methods and no education on abdance of rules and regulations. So, our universities should do many things to make our rules and regulations strictly obeyed.

Note:

prevention: 预防

consequence: 后果

##### 技巧点拨：

在讨论过程中，可以就一个方面进行论述，也可以从多个方面辩证地进行论述，只要言之有理就行。另外一点需要指出的是，虚拟语气的使用问题，考生在练习中，尤其要注意培养良好的语言习惯。

A Why did you say that the effectiveness of rigorous punishment methods has been proven by many facts?

OK, I'll give you a good example. Nowadays many countries have made rigorous punishment methods against the violation of rules and regulations, and it has proven to be very effective. For example, if you threw garbage here and there in Singapore, the fine would be very high. So, nobody throws garbage here and there in Singapore now. So, our university should establish rigorous punishment methods to the violator of rules and regulations.

技巧点拨：

这属于常识性的题目，不难回答。只要考生的语言功底

过硬，完全可以举出让人信服的例子来。

☑ Are all the rules and regulations in your university reasonable? (Give one or two examples.)

A Yes, most of the rules and regulations in my university are reasonable. However, some of them are not reasonable and we students often complain about them. Take the rules of electricity as an example. It's right to forbid the use of electricity, but

it's unreasonable to shut off the electricity at 11 o'clock every night. In fact, it's not convenient to our lives especially in summer. We can't use fans without electricity, so we often wake up because of the heat.

技巧点拨：

回答当然是否定，这样的话才会有很多内容可以说。

If any of your classmates did not observe the university rules and regulations, what would you do?

I would tell them the rules and regulations. Other students might not stand up to tell their classmates that they violated some rules or regulations because they are afraid of offending their classmates. In fact, I think they are disingenuous and I would look down upon them. Rules and regulations are made to keep our campus lives in order, and if someone violates them, there must be some other students who would be affected negatively. Besides, most violators are not aware that what they did is a violation. Otherwise, they would not do so. So, we are responsible to tell our classmates the rules and regulations, and I'm not afraid to offend them. On the contrary, I think only by doing so can we be good friends.

Note:

offend:得罪

disingenuous: 无诚意的，虚伪的

技巧点拨：

上题的论述十分充分并且让人信服，原因在于考生在回答问题时结合了自己的切身体会，真正从自身角度来进行论述。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cafc584-3534-47ac-9ac9-83d046616970/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_335_1265_876_1619.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2F8b67f1b05b2edbf0d2ace0c6a65a9b5850d64ad75120c6de82c66dd6bd78fab7" alt="Image" width="45%" /></div>


My picture is a scene in a library. In the library, there are many books on the shelves with many students sitting beside the tables reading. On the wall, we can see a notice board on which there is the word "Keep Quiet". But there are two boys who are chatting loudly, and other students seem annoyed with the two boys. As everybody knows, we should keep quiet in the library. Otherwise, we would disturb other students. This is a rule established by every university. We all should keep quiet in the library even if there is not such a rule. The two noisy students are uncivil and in bad taste. I don't like such students, and I would not do such a thing. If there is someone making noises in the library, I'd try to stop him.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cafc584-3534-47ac-9ac9-83d046616970/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_259_560_889_903.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A57Z%2F-1%2F%2F2cd7c467c2a1a58bb9db70c7b2734c9595622e3e716132f2c2e5faf523eb5283" alt="Image" width="52%" /></div>


Note:

noticeably:醒目地 uncivil:不文明的 in bad taste:粗鲁的,没有礼貌的

Candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

As adults, should university students be responsible for their own behavior? Why?

Yes. University students are all adults, so they should be responsible for their own behavior. As adults, they are no longer children and so should not be short of judgment and self-discipline. If their activities are unmerited, maybe many people would be affected negatively. Therefore, adults should take both themselves and other people into consideration before they do something. They are responsible for their own behavior and so are our university students.

Note:

unmerited: 不当的

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

In the dormitory, two girls are cooking something with an electric cooker on a table. A fan is blowing beside these two girls. Behind them, there are two beds made of wood. This is very dangerous. If the electric cooker was not working

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//2a66677a-5ca9-4931-8ba9-016380cfc2de/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_307_176_937_515.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A42Z%2F-1%2F%2F22570590fc0f8663421d179d5a1dd44db7a8c7a262b1caea281bb2db00340cbf" alt="Image" width="52%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">In the dormitory</div> </div>


properly it could catch on fire. All the materials made of wood or cloth such as the table, bunk bed and curtains would also catch fire. There are many students living in the dormitory building, and it would be difficult for them to flee from the building once it caught fire. Therefore, although cooking is an enjoyable thing for most girls, they should not cook in the dormitory for the sake of safety.

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think that it is nice to live off Campus?

B Yes, of course. If I can rent an apartment outside of Campus, I'll enjoy my studies and my life. I can cook anytime I want. I can watch TV, and I don't have to worry about being noisy, and being late.

技巧点拨：

题目不仅要求对图片进行描述，还要求对图片中违反校规的行为做出评价。考生不要忽视了后者，或者是只顾描述，到最后没有时间进行评价。

# 口试全真预测试题(二)及答案

##### 【主题】 $ \underline{\text{Campus life}} $

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

How do you like campus life? (Why do you think so?)

The campus life is interesting and challenging, so I enjoy it. Above all, campus life is an important stage in my life. It's entirely different from the life in high school. Now, I live at a dormitory with my classmates, while in high school. I lived with my family. Everything seems fresh to me when I began my campus life. The campus is very large and there are many buildings. I was excited and curious when the first class began. I had a great number of new schoolmates. In the library, there are so many books. I was determined to study hard. There are so many extracurricular activities on campus, and I took part in several of them. Like Newton, I'm searching for beautiful shells on the beach of knowledge.

Note:

shell: 贝壳

beach:海滩

extracurricular: 课外的

技巧点拨：

所有类似 How do you like ...? 的问题；考生在回答时一定要表达出自己的喜好，如 I like it, I don't like it, I hate it 等。

What do you think is the most attractive aspect of campus life?

The most attractive aspect of my campus life, I think, is to take part in rich extracurricular activities in our spare time. In our spare time, we play badminton, table tennis, football, and volleyball, any kind of sports we like. Also, we have a lot of contests such as speech contests and debates. And for music lovers, there is a Music Association in which they practice singing and appreciate great musical works together. Actually there are about one hundred of student associations on campus. In a word, there are numerous extracurricular activities on campus. I can learn much from them and my life is colorful and exciting because of them.

Note:

student association: 学生社团

技巧点拨：

在举出最富有吸引力的方面之后，需要用大量的例子来进行论证。

##### Do you find it easy to get along with your classmates? (Why or why not?)

Yes, it's easy to get along with my classmates. Although there were some problems among us, we solved them finally. Actually all my classmates are considerate and helpful. They can understand other classmates easily, and help the classmates who are having difficulties and troubles. Certainly, our characteristics and personalities are different, so sometimes there will be some disagreements among us. But if we are confronted with problems, we'll have a discussion to solve them. I'm so fortunate to have such classmates as them.

##### Can you say something about the living conditions on campus?

All students are complaining about the living conditions on campus, so do I. Firstly, our dormitory is too crowded. The room is only five meters long and three meters wide, but there are six students sharing a room. We have no personal room expect for our beds. Secondly, the condition of the classrooms is bad, too. The doors are noisy when they are open or closed. There are no fans in the classrooms, so it is very hot with more than one hundred of students having their class in a room. It's also very cold in winter. Our library is air-conditioned, so every student wants to study in the library. The result is that it's very crowded in the library. We are expecting our university to invest more money in improving the living conditions, because we can't put up with it any longer.

Note:

complain about:抱怨 put up with:忍受

技巧点拨：

在论述自己的观点的时候，如果结合了自己的切身体会的话，一般能使人非常信服。

Do you find life on campus convenient? Please give your reasons.

Certainly it's very convenient if you live on campus. Firstly, your dormitory is near the classroom, library, dining halls and so on. You can go everywhere on campus on foot or by bicycle. You need not take a motorcycle or ride a bicycle for over ten minutes to do something. Secondly, in my university, the Internet is free. You can download your favorite movies and music from some sites. But if you live off campus, you can't share these conveniences. Finally, living in dormitory with your classmates is more convenient to get information on lectures, jobs and so on, which is very important to everybody. So, I like living on campus in spite of the bad living conditions.

##### 技巧点拨：

注意考官在题中已经给出论述原因的要求，实际上，如果没有提示的话，考生也应该论述原因。

##### What should the university do to make campus life more colorful?

The first thing I think is to improve the living and teaching conditions. A comfortable life is the foundation of a colorful campus life. And if everywhere is crowded, you can't concentrate on studying or have a good rest after study. What's more, universities should encourage the development of student associations and hold many kinds of activities such as the contest of "The Top Ten Singers on Campus". Students can get a great deal of joy and knowledge from these activities.

##### Note:

The Top Ten Singers on Campus: 校园十佳歌手

##### What do you think of your teachers and professors? (Please give your reasons)

I'm a third year student, so there are many teachers and professors who have taught me. Some of them are very good. However, some of them are boring. Some teachers and professors have rich teaching experience and they know how to teach. They give us lively examples and case study in class; they organize discussions among us very well. In this way, they can stimulate our imagination and interests, and spontaneously we learn much from the classes.

But, there are some teachers and professors who are very boring. They just stood in front of us, reading what has been clearly written in the textbooks. We were all very sleepy at that time. It's really a waste of time.

技巧点拨：

如果要求考生对某件事情或某些人做出评价的话，可以分角度进行论述。这样可以有更多的内容讲。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//2a66677a-5ca9-4931-8ba9-016380cfc2de/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_408_1348_663_1638.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A56Z%2F-1%2F%2F5d67d58051244958aaf73031bb30f9ad0d2e8955477ab638baba7b9b495c0c31" alt="Image" width="21%" /></div>


playing chess

swimming

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cd76343-3c40-4e02-9810-2ed4caa1e77c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_236_179_1013_510.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A42Z%2F-1%2F%2F4aace37b74edee83b2959de26f8a53a92d633bcfe7c71eb74383355b5abb8ff3" alt="Image" width="65%" /></div>


I think college students should be encouraged to have a hobby. Hobbies rank among life's great pleasures. They provide a change from routine and are usually relaxing, enjoyable, and interesting. They help one maintain a mental and emotional balance.

B Yes, I think so. We can learn a great deal from hobbies, such as geography and history knowledge from the hobby of stamp collecting.

But students' chief task is to study. University offers us many courses, and we should have as many courses as we can. From these courses, we also can learn much knowledge, and the knowledge tends to be more systematic.

B Sorry to interrupt you. I just think there is still so much knowledge you can't learn from courses, no matter how systematic it is. For example, if you go out to have a travel, you'll experience different customs and cultures, which are different from that of courses.

But as far as I know, most college students indulge in Internet. They play games or chat with strangers all the day, so their study is affected seriously by their hobbies.

⑧ Yes, it's a problem. But I think just for this reason the university and college should encourage our students to have several hobbies, lead them to have a right attitude on hobbies.

I couldn't agree more. I think the university and college should teach their students to keep balance between study and hobbies.

I still hold my original opinion. Hobbies have another disadvantage, that is, most of them are costly, so I don't think such hobbies suitable for college students.

Maybe you are right. But I think hobby is a personal business. If you have a hobby, your life will be more colorful. If you have no money, there are still some hobbies that cost nothing. It's up to you.

##### 技巧点拨：

在讨论中，一定要有同意、不同意、打断以及插话。这样讨论的气氛才会活跃起来。

Why did you say that hobbies rank among life's great pleasures?

A hobby can provide us with interest, enjoyment, friendship, knowledge and relaxation. It can help us learn more about ourselves or about the world. It may introduce us to friends who share our enthusiasm and from whom we can also learn. It helps both manual and mental workers relax after periods of hard work. It also offers interesting and enjoyable activities for retirees. Moreover, it is helpful to a person's mental and physical health.

##### Note:

manual:体力的

mental: 脑力的

What's the most popular hobby of your classmates?

What's the most popular hobby of my classmates? I think it is surfing the Internet. Almost every one of my classmates have their own computers. And it's very convenient to use Internet, because it's free and the speed is very fast. They almost spend all of their spare time in surfing the Internet, searching for data and information to write their paper, receiving and sending emails, downloading movies and music, roaming in the BBS, etc. There is so much joy in the Internet, and I like it very much.

##### Note:

##### roam:漫游

##### 技巧点拨：

在第一与第二部分中，会有很多信息对第三部分有所帮助，所以考生一定要注意听每个考生的回答。如果暂时想不起来答案的话，可以先简单重复一下问题，为自己争取时间。

Do you think having a hobby will affect your studies? Please give your reasons.

I don't think so. I can control myself very well, so I can keep balance between study and hobby. And I always look on study as the most important thing in my life. If the burden of study is very heavy, I'll spend almost all my spare time to study. So, having a hobby will not affect my studies, on the contrary, it will be helpful to my study. A hobby can relax me. So, when I'm too tired to study, I'd like to do the things I'm interested in. After that, I will have more energy to study efficiently.

##### Note:

look on ... as... : 把……看作

Some students spend a lot of time surfing the net. Do you think it's a problem?

I think it depends on which kind of things they are doing on the Internet. If they are searching for data and information to write their paper, receiving and sending

emails, downloading movies and educational programs to practice English, roaming in the BBS to get useful information, I think it's helpful to the students' growth, and we should encourage them. But if they spend a lot of time playing games and chatting with strangers on line, and keep their mind idle, I think it's really a problem. Certainly, their friends will give them advices.

##### Note:

download: 下载

idle: 空闲的，懒惰的

技巧点拨：

本题可以从两个角度进行分析。注意考题是一般疑问句，所以一定要做肯定和否定的回答，在回答的过程中相应运用举例的方法，在不知不觉中回答的内容就会丰富起来。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cd76343-3c40-4e02-9810-2ed4caa1e77c/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_446_870_782_1241.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A54Z%2F-1%2F%2F33cdc3790f4e6d668c1fb0847a6b016051bc0a0cba102a2dcf51caf9bbfa165f" alt="Image" width="28%" /></div>


In my hand, the picture shows a girl is playing tennis. Actually it is a kind of physical hobby. Intellectual hobbies such as reading can train one's brain and provide temporary escape from one's trouble. On the other hand, physical hobbies such as playing tennis can keep us fit and develop team spirit. I think, In order to make our lives enjoyable, we should have both intellectual and physical hobbies.

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

Can you say something about your own hobby or hobbies?

My hobby is reading. Reading is one of the greatest pleasures in my life. It increases my pleasure when I'm cheerful, and lessens my troubles when I'm sad. Whenever I read books, they always give me enjoyment and satisfaction. The most valu

able gifts offered by books are: knowledge and experience. In books I may visit famous places, meet various people, get to know new thoughts, which is quite beyond my experience of everyday life. So, I like reading very much.

技巧点拨：

读书是一种很常见的业余爱好，所以，如果在考前有所准备的话，考试就不成问题了。

### OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

surfing(漫游，冲浪) the net(Internet)

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cd76343-3c40-4e02-9810-2ed4caa1e77c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_229_572_506_884.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A59Z%2F-1%2F%2F8d7e781644e35f939fa364b3e3d6ee4f4b31c1d6998bfd80c1bcd473283db0b4" alt="Image" width="23%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cd76343-3c40-4e02-9810-2ed4caa1e77c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_515_544_724_886.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A00Z%2F-1%2F%2F5efcb1e2af1edc5daeeb6c2f8e802daee6887ff4e386cb84030e0dbffea163dd" alt="Image" width="17%" /></div>


going on a tour

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6cd76343-3c40-4e02-9810-2ed4caa1e77c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_739_567_998_880.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A00Z%2F-1%2F%2F38a1fd15a8fe487ee8705cde25bf71285bf592a2672e53cc56500514b6524b0e" alt="Image" width="21%" /></div>


Internet is a fantastic information expressway that connects every corner of the world. However, like everything else, it has both positive and negative aspects. On the one hand, it provides tremendous most updated and useful information. We can not only get all kinds of useful information and educational programs from the long-distance educational system, but also shop on website. On the other hand, there is also some useless or even harmful information. Many students keep playing games or visiting useless websites all the day. It takes them much time and maybe waste them much money.

Listening to music and traveling are two different hobbies. Music, we say, is the art of organizing sounds. Music is a rhythmic poem that inspires, a harmonious tone that tranquilizes, and a melody of mystery and beauty. If you have a hobby of listening to the music, the music would have a wonderful effect on your feelings. Soft and sweet music soothe the wearied, the sad, and the restless; grand music will fill strong men with great ambitions. But only listening to music can't keep one fit and develop team spirit. As for travel, it can keep you fit. Moreover, you can learn much from traveling, and travel can broaden your perspective. However, travel is costly in both time and money.

Note:

rhythmic: 有节奏的，合拍的

soothe: 平静，安慰，使减轻

harmonious: 和谐的，协调的

wearied: 疲倦的，厌倦的

tranquilizes: 使安静，使平静

perspective: 视野

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

Some people spend a lot of money on hobbies. Do you think it worthwhile?

I think it depends on whether you are rich. I mean, if you are rich, you spend a lot of money on hobbies and it brings you happy, it's worthwhile; if you are poor, you spend so much money on your hobbies that you can't dress warmly or eat your fill, I think it is not worthwhile. You should keep balance between the expenditure of hobbies and the joy brought by your hobbies. Although hobby is very important to one's life, there are other aspects that are more important than it.

Note:

dress warmly, eat one's fill: 温饱

技巧点拨：

就业余爱好来说，考试中很有可能出这方面的题目，所以考生可以专门针对几个比较普遍的业余爱好进行准备。

#### 口试全真预测试题(三)及答案

##### 【主题】 Social changes

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Could you say something about the changes that have taken place in your hometown in the last ten years?

There are so many changes in my hometown that have made my hometown quite different from that of ten years before. During the last ten years, China has carried out reforms in various fields such as economics, education and politics. Reforms have already brought Chinese people a lot of benefits, and my hometown is one of the beneficiaries. Firstly, the living conditions have improved greatly. Ten years ago, almost every family in my hometown was very poor, and they could only dress warmly or eat their fill. But now, the lives of the people in my hometown can be summarized by one word: rich. Every family has their own storied buildings with many kinds of electronics instead of an old and crowded bungalow. Moreover, most children of my hometown are educated better than ever before. More and more children are entering universities and colleges to receive higher education. So many changes have taken place in the last ten years, and I can't mention them one by one because of the time limit. I wish you could come to my hometown to experience the changes yourself.

Note:

beneficiary: 受益者

bungalow:平房

##### 技巧点拨：

从以上参考答案中，我们可以看到考生可以很流畅地用英语将其家乡的变化娓娓道来，这要靠考生平时的积累与锻炼。锻炼多了，说出来的东西就更容易让人信服。另外一点需要注意的是，描述过去的事情时，一定要用过去式。

##### Will the increasing number of private cars cause any problems in big cities?

Certainly the increasing number of private cars will cause some problems in big cities. Firstly, it will increase the pressure in traffic of big cities. Nowadays almost every big city is trying their best to construct streets, highways and subways, but the development of these still can't meet the increasing demand of private cars.

Secondly, the increasing number of private cars will cause increased pollution. Because of the increase of private cars, more and more poisonous gas will be given off to pollute the atmosphere and cause actual harm to the health of people.

技巧点拨：

这类问题应该不难回答，因为考生比较熟悉。阐述时建议使用 Firstly，Secondly 等词语，这样的回答条理比较清晰，也利于考生把握谈几个方面的内容。

#### E What changes will the wide use of the Internet in China bring about?

With the development of personal computers, Internet is becoming more and more popular on the globe with each passing day. The Internet is a powerful way to communicate check emails, get instant message and chat in chat rooms. It has, and will bring China many miracles. Above all, the economy of China will be improved greatly by the wide use of the Internet. Now there is a “New Economy”—one characterized by the dominance of high-technology industries, immunity from cyclical downturns, and, most of all, rapid productivity growth. China will step into a new epoch by the wide use of the Internet. Secondly, it will bring Chinese people changes in lifestyle. With Internet, it is more convenient and faster for people to get a variety of information and all they need to do is just press a few buttons. E-mail can transmit a message quickly, safely and accurately; doctors may use it to diagnose and treat their patients by discussing or exchanging experience with doctors in other parts of the world; students may obtain new knowledge from a national-long-distance-educational-system via Internet; housewives will buy commodities at low prices and of good qualities.

In a word, everybody in China is anticipating the changes brought about by the wide use of the Internet.

Note:

New Economy: 新经济 epoch: 时代

immunity: 免疫力 lifestyle: 生活方式

cyclical downturns: 周期性经济下滑 via: 通过

productivity: 生产率

技巧点拨：

在以上回答过程中，考生运用了自己的专业知识——新经济。所以说，口语考试中，如果考生能用很新或很独特的东西来论证观点或阐述某项事物，会给人耳目一新的感觉。

##### What, in your opinion, is the biggest change that has taken place in China today?

I think the development of the economy is the biggest change that has taken place in China today. In the last ten years, the development of the economy can be seen obviously from many fields such as people's living conditions. There are more elec-

tronics in Chinese homes; more people choose to travel which is costly as their entertainment during their holidays. As everybody knows, economy is the foundation of other aspects of a society. So, the development of the economy promoted the development of culture, military affairs, spaceflight, etc. Now China is a powerful country in the world. More people begin to appreciate and show interest in China.

##### 技巧点拨：

众所周知，经济基础决定上层建筑，所以在回答发生的最大变化或最具影响力的变化时，应该运用这些基本知识。

##### Please tell us something about the changes in higher education in recent years.

The first and biggest change, I think, is that colleges and universities are now enrolling more students than ever before, so more high school students can receive higher education. As a result, more talented people can contribute to the development of the economy. Secondly, the tuition system of higher education is reforming. That means, the tuition and fees are increasing, so the students who passed the University Entrance Exam have the new anxiety of paying college tuition and fees. Many people are complaining about it. However, it will never keep poor students away from university, and I think it will help the development of education industry.

##### 技巧点拨：

在发言的过程中，考生要熟练使用各种关联词，如 but，in spite of，while 等，这对于考试过程中更为生动的表达非常

有用，也使整个表述不至于平淡无味。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

I think it is the changes that have taken place in accommodations that has had the greatest influence on people's lives. Housing is the most important thing to a family. There have been many novels reflecting this phenomenon. For example, in a novel there was a man who couldn't marry a girl who he loved deeply. The reason was that he had no housing, so the girl's family rejected their marriage.

B Oh, I have read such novels. But in my opinion, it's the changes taken place in transportation that have the greatest influence on people's lives. If the transportation was still the same as that of early 80's, the pace of people's lives would be restrained by heavy traffic. It would be very difficult to go to anywhere. And light traffic can help people work or study comfortably. So, I think it's the most influential aspect.

But I think transportation is only one aspect of people's lives. What do you think about it?

Oh, I agree with you. From my point of view, changes that have taken place in

food are the most influential aspect. To human beings, food is a basic necessity. Now, we can eat almost all kinds of fruits no matter which season it is, and it is convenient to buy tasty and nutritious food in supermarket. I think the changes in food better the life of Chinese.

But accommodation is also one of the basic demands of human beings. Moreover, it's a social demand. Living in a commodious home, a family can lead a happier life. So it is a influential aspect.

But do you think transportation is also one of the basic demands of human beings?

A Yes, of course it is. Dress, food, accommodation and transportation are the four basic demands of human beings. They are all very important, and the changes taken place in these aspects are all influential to people's lives. Without any one of them, people would be in trouble. So China should improve these aspects at the same time.

##### 技巧点拨：

在描述事物发展过程的时候，不可避免地要用到过去式，这是需要引起考生注意的地方。另外，有的地方要用到虚拟语气。在平时的英语训练中，要多注意这些方面，以免考试的时候出错。

What kind of housing does your family have? How does your family like it?

Now my family lives in an apartment about one hundred square meters, and there are two bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen and a bathroom in it. My parents, my sister and I all like it very much. I still remember the housing my family used to live in. It was so crowded, and there was only a living room, a bedroom, a kitchen and a bathroom in it. So my sister and I had to live in one room, and my parents in the living room. But now, my sister and I have our own room respectively. And after supper, all my family would sit on the sofa to watch television. It's so happy.

##### Note:

square meter: 平方米

##### 技巧点拨：

在回答问题的时候，可以稍加展开。比如此题问的是现在的居住条件如何，以及对其有何意见，考生自然可以结合以前的居住条件进行论证。

B Is it now more convenient to contact your friends than before? (Please give your reasons.)

With the improvement of accommodation, maybe you are far away from your friends now. If you want to have a face-to-face talk with your friends, it's not as convenient as before. But if you contact your friends in other ways, it's more convenient than before. With the popularization of the telephone and the computer,

now you can contact with your friends by telephone or email. These two ways of communication are all fast and convenient.

##### 技巧点拨：

在对一个问题进行评论的时候，可以分角度进行评论。从某种角度，是一个答案；但从另一个角度，也许又是另外一个答案，这样考生也可以丰富谈话的内容。

### Nowadays there are more and more fast-food restaurants. What do you think of fast food?

I think fast food reflects the development of our society. It's a good thing. First of all, people now are too busy with their work to spare time for a meal. Therefore, they choose to look for the most convenient food at hand to save time for more work. The second reason is that fast-food is cheaper. You need not empty your wallet to buy a meal. Today, many kinds of food can be converted into fast food. In addition to the most popular ones such as hot dogs and hamburgers, rice and noodles can be also served as fast food. Maybe some people would complain that fast food is short on nutrition, but I think the choice is up to you. You need not eat fast food if you don't like it.

##### Note:

noodle:面条

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Leisure Activities early 80's</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1be1ca27-9bc8-4f10-a055-adea4afca3ed/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_271_1152_897_1463.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A36Z%2F-1%2F%2F92ff1cc360c7556590c9a8b0954622b5ef5a6a159a41be5e7d446d31629857b1" alt="Image" width="52%" /></div>


The card in my hand shows the changes in people's leisure activities in the last twenty years. In the early 80's, most families were very poor, and TV sets were rare at that time. Maybe there is only one family that could afford a TV set in a whole town, so all villages would go to that family to watch TV. It was crowded in the room, and it was also very interesting with so many people watching one television. Another leisure activity at that time was to see a hypaethral film on a

playground in summer. Finally, in their spare time, people had no plaza or commercial centre to go shopping. They just bought goods in small shops usually with a salesperson standing behind the counter. But now, as the accommodation becomes a social demand in 90's, there are many kinds of leisure activities we can take part in. We can watch color television with our family, and it's very comfortable to sit on the sofa, chat with our parents when watching television. Also, in our spare time we can go out to practice singing in the Karaoke room with our friends. Moreover, you can make a travel for a week-long holiday. It helps to better your physical health and your perspective. More and more families and students choose travel as their entertainment in holidays. In general, there are many more leisure activities than there were in the 80's.

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

☑ Why did you say that accommodation is a social demand?

Oh, I think it's very easy to explain. Everyone wants to have a commodious house, because it makes his or her life more comfortable. If you had no housing you would hesitate to marry. A commodious and bright house makes your family happier. So, I said that accommodation is a social demand.

技巧点拨：

这里同样要注意虚拟语气和过去式的问题。

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1be1ca27-9bc8-4f10-a055-adea4afca3ed/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_255_978_591_1323.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A38Z%2F-1%2F%2F728da1a6db879b25de16cf44b4afe9b5678d2590de60c2a72e32ba0aa7e78f3b" alt="Image" width="28%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1be1ca27-9bc8-4f10-a055-adea4afca3ed/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_620_974_915_1319.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A38Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe1140ccb5f5ec00072aab033fecd12fa1e56d48c1c501a89daf8e5e27647a3d9" alt="Image" width="24%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1be1ca27-9bc8-4f10-a055-adea4afca3ed/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_311_1367_588_1651.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A38Z%2F-1%2F%2F716f65b9f19d5ea8f5e26c232753c7e57a06d73ada73b63cfb87607903db94c5" alt="Image" width="23%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1be1ca27-9bc8-4f10-a055-adea4afca3ed/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_642_1339_911_1654.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A38Z%2F-1%2F%2F66d88fddab52bad011b77d0df05804b77d32ca9787eea310a17f7aaefa5a5ff6" alt="Image" width="22%" /></div>


During the twenty years since 80's, great changes have taken place in transportation. The above picture shows the transportation of early 80's. Some people are riding their bicycles; some people are walking. There are many people standing at the bus stop to wait for their buses, and the arriving bus is already very crowded. There is only one car running in the street. At that time, the Chinese motor vehicle industry was not well developed, and bicycles were one of the most popular vehicles. With the development of the economy, transportation is also improved greatly in 90's. Our country invested much money into the construction of transportation. The streets are widened, and there are more and more streets, highways and subways in China. In the second picture, two girls are waiting for a bus at the bus stop, and there are several buses and many cars running in the wide street. It reflects the changes taken place in transportation, compared with the first picture.

Another picture is about a hypaethral bazaar. A housewife is buying food with a basket in her hand. It's sunny and hot, and maybe the basket is very heavy. The second picture is about a supermarket in the late 90's. There are many kinds of fruits, fishes and so on in the supermarket. The commodity is placed on the shelves in order. A woman is pushing a pushcart in which there are many things. These two different pictures show the changes that have taken place in the food, both in its varieties and the way to buy it.

Note:

rare: 稀罕的，珍贵的

clothespress: 衣橱

hypaethral: 露天的

shabby:破旧的

commodious: 宽敞的

utensils: 器具

bazaar: 集市

Do you think it's a good idea to replace low buildings with tall buildings in big cities? (Why?)

I think it's a good idea. There are too many people in big cities. But the tract of cities is limited, so we should build more housing on the limited tract to meet the increasing demand of increasing numbers of people. There is only one way, that is, to replace low buildings with tall buildings. Of course, there are some disadvantages in doing so. For example, it's difficult to climb stairs if you live on the top floor. But in general, the advantages outweigh the disadvantages and it's the only way to accommodate so many people in big cities.

Note:

tract: 土地

##### 技巧点拨：

在对一个问题进行评论的时候，要将其优点和缺点都说到，并且要指出是利大于弊还是弊大于利，从而提出自己的观点。

#### 口试全真预测试题(四)及答案

##### 【主题】 Information and Modern life

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Where do you usually get the latest news? From newspapers, the radio, TV or the Internet?

I usually get the latest news from the newspaper and the Internet. I'm a university student and I live on campus. I have my own computer. Every day I spend almost all my spare time in Internet to search for data and information to write papers, visit some websites to get the latest news and so on. For me, Internet access is free. So I can get the latest news freely. But if I want to know details about some news of special topics, I'll buy newspapers.

技巧点拨：

在回答问题的时候，最好结合自己的亲身体会，这样能让人信服。

E What newspapers are available on campus?

There are two news stands on campus in my university. They sell all kinds of newspapers, such as Nanfang Weekly, the People's Daily and so on. There are also various kinds of entertainment newspapers, academic newspapers like 21st Century Economy Report and so on. It's very convenient to get many kinds of newspapers. For this reason, I like campus life.

Note:

Nanfang Weekly:《南方周末》

The People's Daily:《人民日报》

21st Century Economy Report:《21世纪经济报道》

How often do you watch TV? What programs do you like best? (Please give your reasons.)

There is no TV set in my dormitory, so I seldom watch TV since I began my campus life. But I used to watch TV very often, and my favorite program was network news offered by CCTV. Firstly, it covers news about important events both at home and abroad. Secondly, the news it provides is timely and reliable. Final

ly, it's broadcasting in the evening during the so-called "golden time". For these reasons, the network news program reaches a wide range of viewers.

##### 技巧点拨：

在描述自己喜欢的电视节目的时候，可以说自己一直很喜欢的某个电视节目，也可以分阶段说自己以前喜欢什么，现在又喜欢什么，并逐一结合自己的实际情况说明理由。

Do you have access to the Internet on campus? Which sites do you often visit and why?

Yes, I have access to the Internet on campus. I always search for data and information, receive and send e-mails, get the latest news and chat with my good friends online. The internet is indispensable to my campus life. It makes my life rich and colorful. I often visit www.sina.com.cn and www.hotmail.com. I often check my e-mail boxes, because almost all my friends live in other cities, provinces or even abroad. I communicate with them briefly by e-mails. And I'm holding a part time job as a translator. My boss sends me the papers needing to be translated and I send him the papers that have been translated into Chinese. So, every day I check my email boxes at least three times.

##### How does the mass media affect our lives?

First of all, the mass media has great influence on our lives. There are so many forms of media in our lives: radio, television, newspaper and Internet. As soon as we open our eyes early in the morning, we will be influenced by some kind of media. Mass media helps us follow the latest development of many aspects in our lives. It helps us know the changes taking place in our country and the world, teaches us knowledge, makes us relaxed and so on. Certainly there are some negative affects of mass media. For example, some news is not objective, or even not true. It is broadcast just for the sake of making profits. For this reason the government should strengthen the supervision of mass media.

##### 技巧点拨：

在参考答案中，既谈到了大众传媒对人们生活的正面影响，也谈到了不利影响，并在最后提出自己的看法，回答得非常充分。

##### What would life be like without the mass media?

If there were no mass media, our lives would be gloomy and humdrum. Because of mass media, we can know the latest development in economics, politics, culture, entertainment, military affairs and so on; we can get useful information to lead a happier life; we can obtain knowledge from the educational programs on television; we can relax from tiresome study or work. Without mass media, maybe you would have no topic when you chat with your friends; we could do nothing after work and study. So the world without mass media is beyond imagination.

Note:

gloomy: 黑暗的，阴郁的

humdrum: 单调的

beyond imagination: 难以想像

技巧点拨：

在陈述过程中，考生可以凭着自己丰富的想像，利用排比句的句式来表达自己的想法。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8ccffc49-c979-4928-983e-fbab9c8cce67/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_235_603_988_1094.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A37Z%2F-1%2F%2F2fc6eb378c8e21d1f20bf4fa7fe353fc46576610a90724b72ec9f5df6e4b26bf" alt="Image" width="63%" /></div>


I think newspaper is the best way to get the latest news. What happens in a day will be found in the newspaper the next day.

⑧ But television can give you the latest news earlier. It's quicker than newspaper.

I think newspaper is the best way to get the latest news. As for TV, whether we like a particular piece of news or not, all we have to do is sit in front of the tube and “let it happen”. But a newspaper reader can select what he is interested in, skip what he thinks is boring or irrelevant and read it anytime he likes.

Yes, what did you said just now is reasonable, but television news can vividly bring into our living rooms dramatic events of singular importance, such as space launchings, natural disasters, and wars, etc.

But it cannot cover important stories in the depth they may deserve because of its time limitation. On the contrary, print news excels in its ability to devote as much space to a story as necessary, though it cannot compete with television visually.

As for me, I think the Internet is the best way to get the latest news. The news on

it can be almost instantaneous especially when a gymnastic contest is held. And there is also enough space to cover anything such as a story. There are also more pictures, which are clearer than those of newspaper.

A Oh, I don't think so. Maybe in the future the Internet will be the best way. But now, there is only a small proportion of people who have computers and access to the Internet. It's not popular, so it's not convenient. But the newspaper is very convenient. In a word, newspaper is the best way to get the latest news.

Note:

tube: 电视机

singular:非凡的

excel in: 在……方面优秀，胜过他人

disaster:灾难

instantaneous: 同步的，等时的，即时的，即刻的，立即发生的，瞬间的

A Why did you say that the newspaper is very convenient?

B Firstly, you can buy newspaper anywhere at anytime, such as the corner of a street, the subway, the supermarket and so on. Secondly, a newspaper is really very cheap compared with Internet fees. Anyone can afford it no matter how poor he is. It's only about 1 Yuan. Sometimes you can subscribe one of your favorite newspapers, and the newspaper will be sent to your home every day. Then you need not go out to buy newspapers.

技巧点拨：

在回答问题的过程中，如果觉得没有太多的内容可讲的话，考生可以适当地将话题进行扩展延伸，这也是一个技巧。

Which newspaper do college students like most? And why?

I just think different students like different newspapers. Sports fan like Sports Weekly most. Students majoring in Economics like 21st Century Economy Report most. Students majoring in International Politics like People's Daily. Last term, I was the manager of a campus newsstand, so I know which one is the best seller. Besides the newspapers mentioned above, Nanfang Weekly is another newspaper which is popular among college students. The reason is that this newspaper always discloses something secret in economics or politics such as bribery.

Note:

disclose: 揭露

技巧点拨：

这个问题可以说是大学生活的一部分，只要平时注意观察，回答起来并不是很难。

A Which kind of mass media do you think is most influential, TV, newspapers, the radio or the Internet? (Give your reasons.)

I think newspaper is the most influential mass media. Firstly, the news of TV and

radio are subjective and adiaphorous, but in the news of newspaper, the author can express his attitude or opinion towards events through literature. In fact, the newspaper is full of opinions on movie star, football star, famous people and political events. Some newspapers are famous for their critical positions on many events. Nanfang Weekly is a good example. The news on Internet has its own characteristics and sometimes it's even more detailed than in newspapers. But it is not very popular nowadays, so what people talk about in their office or home is always the viewpoint of some newspapers.

Note:

adiaphorous: 不偏不倚的，中立的

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//7f6ca9cb-4c23-4c95-b260-604fad533714/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_430_750_797_1036.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A45Z%2F-1%2F%2Fbc49fcd3471aba517dddbca265ac1afd35905c815d3f13a35f4ae306ebfc2a08" alt="Image" width="30%" /></div>


My picture shows a kind of mass media—newspaper. In my picture, some students are reading the newspaper placed on the bulletin board. There are numerous kinds of newspapers which involve in almost every aspect of our lives, such as economy, politics and so on. It's very convenient to buy newspapers, at the corner of street, in the supermarket, in the subway, etc.. And newspaper offers you great deal of information.

Note:

bulletin board: 公告栏

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think newspapers will be replaced by the Internet in the future? Why or why not?

The news on the Internet has almost all the strong suits of newspaper. But I don't think newspaper will be replaced by the Internet in the future. Firstly, the news on the Internet has almost all the strong suits of that of newspaper. For example,

it has enough space to be devoted to a story. There are all kinds of attitudes towards the events offered by Internet, and you can also take part in discussions that are very interesting. Secondly, you need not buy newspapers every day. Although it's very cheap to buy a newspaper, if you keep buying a newspaper every day for several years, the expenditure will be considerable. Internet is comparatively cheaper. Finally, it will not cause the waste of natural resource or environment damage, because paper is made from wood. The Internet is helpful to environmental protection.

Note:

strong suit: 强项，优点

considerable: 相当可观的

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//7f6ca9cb-4c23-4c95-b260-604fad533714/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_432_707_737_948.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A47Z%2F-1%2F%2F4c8659187c7929ef7fe3b42f1bb02c42e762d9f78f7156d7ef4ed61a707f065c" alt="Image" width="25%" /></div>


In my picture, a boy is surfing on Internet to get the information he needs. Internet is another important kind of mass media. You can visit numerous websites such as a mail site, a public agency, and a research agency. From Internet, you can get detailed information. For example, it's very easy to get information on KFC. Just put in the key word like KFC, and then the search engine will give many sites on KFC. What you need to do is just to click and visit these sites, and it's OK. Besides, the information is divided into many catagories. If you want to know something about mobile, there are several excellent websites. You can get acquainted with the price, strong suits and shortcomings of every kind of mobile that can't be found in television or in the newspapers.

Note:

placard: 张贴

##### KFC: 肯德基

Thank you. Now, Candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think it is necessary to read newspapers when you can get the news on Internet?

For most of us today, Internet has become our main source of daily news. But I think it's still necessary to read newspapers. It's true that Internet news can vividly

ly bring into our living rooms dramatic events of singular importance, but it cannot cover important stories in the depth they may deserve because of its time limitation. On the contrary, print news excels in its ability to devote as much space to a story as is necessary, though it cannot compete with Internet visually. Besides, Internet is essentially a passive medium. Most importantly, if all of us get news and information exclusively from television, there will be a decline in general literacy. By contrast, although following and absorbing a newspaper article is a little bit hard as it requires a high level of mental involvement, it helps to develop one's intellectual skills. So, it's not necessary to read newspaper when we can get the news on Internet.

##### 技巧点拨：

考题的要求是对你自己的图片进行描述，不需要对别人手中的其他图片进行描述，这是尤其需要注意的地方。另外，在描述图片的时候一定要仔细观察，比如第一幅图片中，电视里的人物是非常重要的，考生在描述中不能忽视这一点。

# 口试全真预测试题(五)及答案

##### 【主题】 Education in China

##### Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Which do you think is more important for university students, practical skills or theoretical knowledge? Give your reasons.

As for me, I think practical skills and theoretical knowledge are both important. In another word, the most important thing for university students is to learn how to put their theoretical knowledge into practice. So theoretical knowledge is the foundation for the development of practical skills, and practical skills are the final goal. These two aspects are both of vital importance for university students. Without either of them, the campus life is a failure, and the higher education is a failure to some extent.

##### Note:

to some extent: 从某种角度，多少有一点

##### 技巧点拨：

本题是二选一的题目，考生没有从其中选择，而是将二者综合起来，得出一个全新的答案。在考试中，最好是选出其中一个。但是考生如果觉得有更好的答案，而且能够自圆其说的话，也可以像本题这样回答，论证得有理有据的话，还会赢得考官的好感。

Today university students have to pay tuition and fees. Do you think this is reasonable? (Why?)

It's a fact that university students nowadays have to pay tuition and fees. Moreover, the tuition and fees are increasing year by year. I think it's reasonable, although some people are complaining that some poor students can't afford the high tuition and fees and were kept away from university. In my opinion, the new tuition system does add many difficulties to the poor students but it will never keep them away from university. Firstly, the government has provided some special assistance for poor students. Secondly, they are entitled to take a loan from banks or work some part time jobs during their spare time. Finally, they can try to win a scholarship by additional effort. However, with more income, universities can invest much more money to improve the living and teaching conditions, and better the quality of higher education. It's very important to the development of universities, higher education and our country.

Note:

year by year: 逐年地，一年又一年地

技巧点拨：

上述答案针对大学新的收费政策，既承认其缺点，也指出了其优点，并在最后提出利大于弊的结论，论证得非常有力。

What's your major? Why did you choose it?

My major is International Trade. Before the entrance exam to college, I had considered this question for a long time. That time China was trying to become a member of WTO, and the entry was widely expected. I thought China would enter into WTO in two years, and last year China entered into WTO as I had expected. More multinational corporations and capital will be lured into China, and the amount of trade between China and other countries will be larger and larger. So, I chose international trade as my major. Now I'm more and more interested in international trade, and my knowledge of this field is becoming richer and richer. I'm confident that I can do a good job with my ability in the future.

##### 技巧点拨：

此题相当于自我介绍，只是问题涉及得较为深入而已。考生在考试之前，首先要准备一个自我介绍，考试开始的时候考官会要求每个人做自我介绍。另外，在准备自我介绍的时候，考生不妨将其中的各个方面展开一下，以防考试中会被问到。

Apart from your major, what other subjects do you think are very important for you?

Please explain briefly.

My major is International Trade, and I like this major very much. Apart from my major, I think finance is also very important for me. International Trade and Finance are part of economy. As everybody knows, all courses in one area have some internal connections with each other, especially in science courses. The subject of Finance will help me study International Trade. Secondly, the employees of many multinational corporations are required to obtain knowledge of many fields especially Finance. So, it's very important for us to get a position in these multinational corporations. And working for a multinational corporation is one of my dreams. I'd like to learn some courses of Finance.

##### 技巧点拨：

在回答这个问题的时候，考生可以选择与自己的专业联系比较紧密的学科。

Would you like to be a teacher after you graduate? Give your reasons.

In the past three years of my college life I have never ceased to think what kind of work I shall take up upon graduation. Although my ideas are not consistent, I

have now decided on a college teacher as my life long career. A number of reasons have led me to choose this occupation. Firstly, teaching is learning. Secondly, teaching means freedom and independence. As a teacher, I'm free to use my own ideas and make my own decisions. Finally, I like teaching because it offers a certain peace of mind. No more rush to catch a morning bus, no more anxiety to please a boss, no more worries about your paycheck which is steady, if not handsome.

技巧点拨：

teacher 是一种比较好谈的工作，考生也可以选择自己比

较熟悉的职业来谈，比如说与自己的专业有关的职业。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to discuss about the two ways of learning English—group discussion and English class. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

I think the best way is group discussion. In my English class, my English teacher often let us discuss on some special topic. During the discussion, I make a presentation on my opinion and debate with others to clarify it. The atmosphere of class is very active, and we all readily learn English in this way. Our listening and speaking are both improved greatly.

But I'm afraid your English is not idiomatic. Maybe there are some problems in your pronunciation, and if so, you might be misunderstood by foreigners. From this angle, I think language lab is the best way.

In this way, your listening ability can be improved greatly, but not your speaking English.

B Yes, I couldn't agree more. Listening and speaking are both important to English learning.

So, I think group discussion is the best way.

B Oh, I'm sorry to interrupt you. In my opinion, English corner is the best way. When you talk with a native English speaker, you can greatly improve your listening and speaking English.

But usually the English corner is held once a week. English learning needs to be practiced frequently.

From my point of view, these ways are all very important. As for an English learner, he should learn grammar and words, practice listening and speaking. The final goal of English learning is to use English fluently. So you should keep these ways in balance, and you can't learn English well only by using one or two of these ways.

##### ☑ Why did you say that you don't like big class?

There are several reasons. First of all, it's tiresome and boring to take notes all

the time in English class. And culture is neglected in the class. We even don't know how to communicate with others in English after ten years of study. It's really a failure of the university English teaching. So, this kind of teaching should be reformed.

A Could you say something about the way your English class is conducted? How do you like it?

In my English class, my English teacher often organizes discussion among the classmates. Before we have a discussion, the teacher will divide us into several groups and give us the topic to be discussed in the next class. Then the students will begin to prepare for the topic in the following days. Every student is very active in discussion. Students will clarify their point by presentation and arguing with other students. Our listening and speaking ability can both be improved greatly in this way. So, I think it's a good way to learn English especially for university students.

##### 技巧点拨：

注意题目不仅要求学生对自己的英语课老师上课的方式进行描述，还要表明是否喜欢这种方式。

#### B How did you learn to speak English?

Two years ago, I learned to speak English chiefly by group discussion in my English class and English corner. My English teacher would give us a topic before discussion. I was very active and liked to argue with others to clarify my viewpoint. Every Saturday, I would go to the English corner and talk with some native English speakers. In the English corner, I got to know my good friend Mike, who is an American. We are close friends now and I practice my English by talking with him in English.

##### 技巧点拨：

对于学习英语的方法，应该有很多可说。上述答案中，分了两个阶段，先是通过上课讨论和英语角，之后则是交了个外国朋友，通过与外国朋友聊天来提高英语，回答得比较巧妙。

In some universities, multimedia is used in teaching English. What do you think of this form of teaching?

I think it's a very good thing, and all the university students will be happy to experience this change in English teaching system. Before the exercise of multimedia, we can only listen to the teacher or have a discussion with our classmates in English class. If we want to listen to the native English or talk with native English speakers, we have to listen to the tape recorder or go to English corner. However, by multimedia, we can watch English educational program or English movies in class, and the teacher can teach students according to the program or movie. This kind of facility

can stimulate students' interest by offering them vivid images and sound. It's of great help to English learning; so nowadays more and more university classrooms are equipped with advanced multimedia.

##### 技巧点拨：

题目是问考生对于多媒体技术在大学英语教学中的应用的看法。所以。考生可以从各个角度来谈自己的看法，比如通过对比以前和现在的英语学习方式，谈多媒体应用的好处。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8d3383c8-5a9f-4870-86f6-4e200fe14540/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_296_629_874_1004.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2Ffb98e3c193029b7593b7f247144f90b883438d4cbfd4e28ef38f02c07079213e" alt="Image" width="48%" /></div>


In my picture, the students are sitting in the classroom, and listening to the teacher carefully. I think maybe what the teacher is talking about is the grammar. In fact, it's a teacher-centered class. The teacher is turning a language class into an information class. It's very common in the university classroom nowadays that the teacher explains every language point in details, while students take notes all the time.

I think this way of learning English is suitable for the primary learners instead of university students. It is not a good way for a university student to learn English, because the students can't practice speaking and listening.

There is an another way of learning English, which is, learning English through group discussion. Every student is very active in discussion. Some of them are talking about their own opinions, and some others are listening to their partners. I think it's a good way to learn English especially for university students. By this way, we can learn how to express ourselves freely in English. Our spoken English can be improved greatly. And it's also a popular way in university English class.

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8d3383c8-5a9f-4870-86f6-4e200fe14540/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_136_1647_165_1674.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2Fd1c5db47dcdbfb3d64353c5e1bd542e771eb2305a11a8c441d7f281a809aaa22" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


What are you going to do to further improve your English?

“There is no end to learning”. Although now I’m good at English, I’d like to do something to further improve my English. Firstly, I will continue to increase my vocabulary and better my grammar by reading English dictionaries and novels. Secondly, I’d like to do some translation work to improve my ability of translation. Thirdly, I want to learn more English of my specialty. So, I’d like to read more papers relevant to my specialty.

Note:

There is no end to learning: 学无止境

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//c1bc6a3d-e8a9-46db-802d-e0311c65e449/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_454_542_767_808.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A45Z%2F-1%2F%2F0176456efcf62bfb536aa8e22bb5d6558ffed8f1a1c7f3ba361d4c932b587130" alt="Image" width="26%" /></div>


My picture shows us a language lab. The teacher is training her students' listening comprehension by English tape. No doubt it is a good way to practice English. This is also the most popular way to learn English. With the development of the economy, almost every university and college has a language lab and even some middle schools have their own language labs.

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

What's your idea of a good English teacher?

A good English teacher should make his English class a student-centered class instead of a teacher-centered class. He should organize all kinds of interesting class activities and make all the students active in these activities. He should always try to instill a love of learning in students and stimulate their interest. As a result, students will find it a pleasure to learn in class by asking questions, discussing or even arguing with the teacher. It's of great importance to a good English teacher. Besides, a good English teacher should be a person with the spirit of devotion, profound updated knowledge and all-round talents.

##### Note:

instill: 慢慢地灌输

技巧点拨：

在这一部分，考官会给每个人相同的四张卡片，但是只需每个人描述其中一幅与自己相对应的图片，不要描述别人的图片，题目要求中考官也强调了这一点。

#### 口试全真预测试题(六)及答案

【主题】 $ \underline{\text{Traffic and Transportation}} $

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Are you satisfied with the traffic conditions in your city? (Why/why not?)

A I'm a university student in Beijing. I'm not satisfied with the traffic conditions in Beijing. The population of Beijing is very large, and it is still increasing now. With the increase of population, the pressure of traffic is heavier and heavier. Beijing has invested much money into construction of transportation, and as a result there are more streets, highways, crossroads and so on. However, the demand of transportation still can't be met. There's always traffic congestion especially in the rush hour. I'd rather not go out if it is rush hour. In order to avoid traffic congestion, I would like to ride a bicycle if the destination is not very far away.

Note:

traffic congestion: 交通堵塞

技巧点拨：

在表明观点之后，一定要通过各种方式来论证自己的观点。

Some people think it's safer to travel by train than by plane. What do you think?

On holidays, especially during summer vacations, I'd like to go outside the city for a change. Nothing can match an airplane for speed. But most important of all, planes have the reputation of being dangerous, and some travelers often feel nervous when they set foot on them. To me, safety is always a top priority. So I will choose to travel by train. Among the various forms of long distance transport, a train is the safest. Even if accident happens, the rate of survival is always higher than that in air accidents.

Note:

match: 比得上，相称

airplane: 飞机

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//c1bc6a3d-e8a9-46db-802d-e0311c65e449/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_128_1571_159_1601.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A46Z%2F-1%2F%2F396257f0fea8063de554843d2a97df8ee5cdb15c4d58a01f17cbcef446bca5aa" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


Do you think it's safe to ride motorcycles? (Why/Why not?)

I don't think it's safe to ride motorcycles. As everybody knows, the speed of motorcycles is much faster than that of bicycles, and almost the same as cars some

times. However, it's really a dangerous vehicle. The most important reason is that there is no special street built for motorcycles, so motorcycles have to share one street with buses, cars and taxis. Another reason is that there are so many vehicles especially in big cities. Sometimes the street is crowded with buses, cars, and taxis. You can't ride a motorcycle at fast speed. Finally, the motorcycle is less stable and there is little protection for the driver in case of an accident. So, I think a motorcycle is a dangerous vehicle.

#### E What traffic problems are common in big cities?

From my point of view, there are two common problems in big cities. The first one is traffic congestion. It's really a headache in every big city like Beijing. In the rush hour, almost every place is crowded with cars, buses and taxis. You may be on or off duty at that time, but you have to sit in buses or taxis quietly no matter how anxious you are. The second one is road accidents. It's also a serious problem. Every day there are several accidents, and maybe one or two people are killed or injured in these accidents.

##### 技巧点拨：

在回答问题的时候，要尽量做到全面。如上题，一般的交通问题都是交通堵塞，考生在回答的时候，也可以联想到交通事故问题。

##### What means of transportation do you often use? (Why?)

I often ride a bicycle. Firstly, I'm a university student, so I have no source of income and chiefly depend on my parents for financial support. I can only afford a bicycle because it is cheap to buy and maintain. Maybe you would advise me to take the bus because it is also very cheap. OK, let's move on to the second reason why I choose a bicycle. By bicycle, I can go anywhere I want to go as long as I'm not tired. It's convenient for riding and parking in crowded streets. If I take a bus, perhaps I can hardly move one step because of the traffic congestion. And another reason is that riding a bicycle is an ideal way of getting daily physical exercise in a big city.

##### 技巧点拨：

从学生的角度来说，自行车是最适合的交通工具。所以考生在回答问题的时候最好选择自行车这一答案，因为它比较易于表达。

##### What do you think of the public transportation in your city?

I'm not satisfied with the public transportation in Beijing. Although there are enough buses for us to go anywhere, and there is also subway in this city, it's still very crowded in the buses and subway. Sometimes I can hardly get on a bus because the bus is too crowded to carry one more person. Standing in the crowded

bus or subway is really an annoying thing, and I hate it. Besides, the construction of transportation can't meet the increasing demand of private cars, buses and taxis. So, traffic congestion often happens in Beijing. If I have to go out to some place, I'd like to ride a bicycle to avoid the traffic congestion.

技巧点拨：

在对一个问题进行评价的时候，考生可以掺入一些个人的感情色彩进去，比如 I hate it 等，会使整个谈话显得生动。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//c1bc6a3d-e8a9-46db-802d-e0311c65e449/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_158_578_1000_1292.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A49Z%2F-1%2F%2F1485c5cc147bb77e4f8659bddf92394fcc29f83eb2120ab656b5ccfe50ee0a9a" alt="Image" width="70%" /></div>


I think the best way to avoid traffic accidents is to obey the traffic rules. As long as you do so, every vehicle can run smoothly and orderly in the street, and no traffic accidents will happen. For example, at the crossing, everyone should obey the traffic lights.

Yes, it's very important to obey traffic rules. But if your vehicle is in poor condition and it breaks down suddenly in the street, a traffic accident will also happen. So, I think we should keep our vehicles in good condition, and it's the best way to avoid traffic accidents.

As far as I know, there are many traffic accidents caused by violation of traffic

rules. Some people ride bicycles with another person sitting at the back seat; some drivers drink too much alcohol before driving; some pedestrians cross the street against the traffic lights.
