B I think we should obey traffic rules to avoid traffic accidents.

But there are still traffic accidents taking place although we obey the traffic rules and keep our vehicles in good condition. If the weather is bad, or if the street is narrow, we can't avoid and traffic accidents. What can we do?

We still can do something to solve this problem. Drivers should keep alert and show consideration for others especially in bad weather and a narrow street.

A Yes. Moreover, pedestrians should also keep alert and show consideration for others. For example, they should look to the left and right when they step off the pavement.

Sure, all of what you said is right. I just think all that you said can be found in the traffic rules. So, as long as we obey the traffic rules, there will be no traffic accidents. It's the best way. Besides, our country should strengthen the supervision of transportation, such as speed limits on the highway in bad weather, broadening the street, forbidding the booths at the sides of streets, etc.

Note:

alert: 提防的，警惕的

pedestrian: 行人

##### 技巧点拨：

有问有答，有插入，有打断，才可以称作是讨论。考生一定要积极参与讨论，这对于总体成绩非常重要。

B What can we do as citizens to help reduce traffic accidents?

I think it depends on the role a citizen plays in the city. If you are a driver, certainly you should not drink alcohol before driving and be aware of the fatal consequences of your carelessness; if you are a pedestrian, then please obey the traffic rules and be careful of the vehicles running in the street. Certainly, no matter which role a citizen plays in his daily life, a driver or a pedestrian, it's a must for him to obey traffic rules.

技巧点拨：

作为一个普通市民，如何用自己的力量来减少交通事故？本题从司机和行人两个角度来回答是值得学习的。考试中还可将这两个方面稍加展开。

What do you think of bicycles as a means of transportation in big cities?

I think bicycle is a good means of transportation in big cities. Firstly, you need not take the crowded bus and subway. You need not wait for your bus at the bus station for a long time. Besides, riding a bicycle can avoid traffic congestion. Some

times a bicycle is faster than a bus or taxi because of the traffic congestion. I think most of us must have had such an experience. And a bicycle is so convenient for riding and parking in crowded streets. You can go anywhere at any time.

技巧点拨：

在历届考题中,有很多次涉及交通问题,所以建议考生针对这一话题进行详细的准备。

##### What measures has the government taken to reduce traffic accidents in cities?

Our government has taken many measures to reduce traffic accidents in cities. Firstly, the government invested much more money in the construction of transportation, such as broadening the streets and building new streets, because traffic accidents are caused by the narrow or crowded streets. Secondly, government has strengthened the supervision of traffic. If it's in bad weather, the speed of vehicles would be limited especially on highways. The government can also invest money into the education to make people conscious of traffic rules. For example, in the street, we can see some advertisements about traffic accidents.

技巧点拨：

考生在回答此类问题时，一定要注意条理性。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a0a3d2aa-8202-40c5-b5d8-562c5a3a8ae5/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_196_1048_578_1375.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A55Z%2F-1%2F%2F47bc218408e8f2037854523720d97961bac041b3b550a098b514e97594c86441" alt="Image" width="32%" /></div>


雾天，汽车追尾

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a0a3d2aa-8202-40c5-b5d8-562c5a3a8ae5/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_618_1025_989_1389.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A55Z%2F-1%2F%2F1984f376b2406911c6de95787061d91558e7fa208d9dc8cf087cfb483cdb3431" alt="Image" width="31%" /></div>


大雨，汽车打滑

There are two pictures in my hand. They show us one of the reasons for traffic accidents —terrible weather such as rain and fog. In the first picture, there are many cars, buses and taxis are running in the smog. But for the drivers, it's too foggy to see other vehicles. Maybe one car wanted to speed up or one bus wanted to slow down, as a result, these two vehicles crashed into each other. And one by one,

the vehicles behind them crashed into each other. In the second picture, it's raining hard, and the street is very slippery. A running car slipped to the side of the street and crashed into a telegraph pole. This scenery frightens a passer-by with an umbrella in his hand.

In the second picture, some people are walking; some people are riding bicycles and motorcycles; some others are in the cars. It's too crowded. Then a car crashed into a fruit booth, and the fruits are dropping down on to the road. The second picture also shows us a narrow street. A line of cars were crossing the street, suddenly a bicycle rushed out and the first car couldn't stop at once. Then they crashed into each other.

##### Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think traffic accidents can be avoided? (Why/Why not?)

Yes, of course traffic accidents can be avoided. Drivers and pedestrians are both responsible for the avoidance of traffic accidents. Firstly, they should both obey traffic rules—no violation of the traffic rules such as traffic lights, no drunk drivers and so on. Secondly, they should both keep alert and show consideration for others. In the street, everybody should be polite to others. Thirdly, everybody should keep their vehicles in good condition.

##### 技巧点拨：

实际上，本题可以从第二部分的讨论中得到很多的提示，所以考生在考试中不要光顾自己想，还要仔细听别人的谈话内容。

### OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Violation of Traffic Rules</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a0a3d2aa-8202-40c5-b5d8-562c5a3a8ae5/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_224_1201_586_1511.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A56Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe6549daba18988db14a18a8d7114fad3d2ac1c65ba1e7c5b89a80dbe77d64b66" alt="Image" width="30%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Poor Condition of Vehicles</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a0a3d2aa-8202-40c5-b5d8-562c5a3a8ae5/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_599_1196_1032_1510.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A57Z%2F-1%2F%2F9d01e4f7a28a1b3a2d27b2ae9d23a415ff67b42bb61ea1ff8d7617eed58b3aeb" alt="Image" width="36%" /></div>


There are many traffic rules. For example, you should not carry passengers when you are riding a bicycle. The first picture in my hand shows the serious consequence of doing so. A man is riding a bicycle and his wife is sitting at the back seat of the bicycle with a child in her arms. But unfortunately the bicycle crashed into a

car, the husband, wife and child are all falling down to the ground.

Poor condition of vehicles is one of the reasons for traffic accidents. In the second picture, many passengers are standing to wait for green light. A boy is riding a bicycle rushing towards an old woman. It seemed that he couldn't stop the bicycle. Then the boy immediately says sorry to the old woman and explains that something has gone wrong with the bicycle brake is out of order.

Note:

slippery: 滑的

booth: 摊子

foggy: 有雾的

brake:刹车

telegraph pole: 电线杆

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think drivers should always be held responsible for traffic accidents? (Why/Why not?)

No, drivers should not always be held responsible for traffic accidents. For some accidents, the drivers are to blame. They sometimes drive too fast and show no consideration for others. They think they are safe at the wheel even though they have drunk too much alcohol. Some drivers are unaware that their carelessness may have fatal consequences. For some accidents, the blame must be put on the pedestrians. Some careless pedestrians step off the pavement without looking to the left or right. Some even cross roads when the traffic lights turn yellow. So, drivers and pedestrians should both be held responsible for traffic accidents.

Note:

fatal:致命的

技巧点拨：

在描述手中的卡片时，考生一定要将卡片中的每一个细节都涉及到，并将卡片中所发生的事情连贯地表达出来。

#### 口试全真预测试题(七)及答案

#### 【主题】 Climate

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

How do you like the weather in Beijing?

A I'm studying at a university in Beijing, and I have been here for three years. Actually I like the weather in Beijing except for its sandstorms. It's always sunny in Beijing, and I like sunny days very much because it makes me happy. My hometown is in the South of China, and is always rainy which is really annoying. I just feel sleepy and it's not convenient to go out in such weather. So, comparing with that of my hometown, I like Beijing's sunny days and dry weather. I don't have chilblains in the winter. Certainly it's annoying when the sandstorms arrive, which bring much inconvenience and trouble to my daily life and study. Generally speaking, I like the weather in Beijing in spite of its sandstorms.

Note:

chilblain: 冻疮 sandstorms: 沙尘暴

技巧点拨：

考生列举了北京的天气，说了北京天气中好的和不好的地方，最后得出还是喜欢北京的天气的结论，论证得较为充分。

People say the climate is becoming warmer. Do you agree? (Why do you think so?)

A Yes, I agree with this point of view. In fact, Beijing is now warmer and warmer with each passing year. I experienced this change during my campus life. Beijing's summer is hotter and hotter, and I hate it. We can't sleep at night because of the heat. The factories and motor vehicles in big cities are giving off a great deal of gases. As a result, the level of greenhouse gases in the atmosphere reaches a new high. For this reason, the average temperature of the world is increasing.

Note:

greenhouse: 温室

技巧点拨：

首先是要对第一个问题做出肯定或否定的说明，之后再说明原因。当然主要原因是温室效应，关键在于考生能否将这个原因表达出来。

What do you think we can do to prevent the climate from becoming worse?

Today, some scientists believe human beings are changing the climate. How can that be? Over the past few centuries, people have been burning more amounts of fuels such as wood, coal, oil, natural gas and gasoline. The gases formed by the burning, such as carbon dioxide, are building up in the atmosphere. They act like a greenhouse. So, reducing the use of fossil fuels would considerably reduce the amount of carbon dioxide produced, as well as reducing the levels of the pollutants which cause acid rain. This can be achieved by either using less energy altogether, or using alternative energy sources. You can help save energy in lots of ways: turn off lights when you leave a room; if you have a car, don't use it for short journeys. Every country is responsible for the supervision of greenhouse gases.

Note:

carbon dioxide: 二氧化碳

considerably: 相当地

技巧点拨：

acid rain: 酸雨

本题不是很好回答，因为涉及较为专业的问题，比如说温室效应、二氧化碳等。所以，考生在平时一定要注重知识的积累。

Is the climate an important consideration when you look for a place to live and work?

Yes, climate is one of the most important considerations when I look for a place to live and work. Before deciding on a university I contemplated this question for a long time. Finally, I decided to work and live in Beijing. It's always sunny and I love sunny days. Because of the dry weather, it is cool in summer and warm in winter than other cities such as Shanghai and Shenzhen. In winter, the temperature indoor is agreeable because there are caliducts in every room.

Note:

agreeable: 宜人的

##### 技巧点拨：

这样的问题很显然要用自己的亲身经历来谈。如果内容稍嫌不够的话，还可以稍微展开一下，比如说除了气候因素之外，还有一些其他的要考虑的因素，当然了，这些内容只能是稍提一下，否则就跑题了。

Do changes in weather affect human emotions?

As far as I am concerned, the weather affects human emotions. For example, I'm very happy on sunny days. If the weather is very good, I like to go out or go shopping with my friends. If it's cloudy or rainy, I would be in no mood for everything. I'd stay at home and sleep. Science has proven that weather can affect human emotions. The air pressure of cloudy or rainy day is higher than that of sunny days. People can feel the change of air pressure, and higher air pressure will make people uncomfortable, which can be seen in their emotions.

Note:

go outing: 出游

air pressure: 气压

技巧点拨：

本题在回答的过程中，考生从自身和科学两个角度证明了天气对人的情绪确实存在影响。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ac7a8aa3-fa7a-412b-95f0-757900c120f8/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_221_545_618_844.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A58Z%2F-1%2F%2F80a35350e44acd0c47e302d1529cf3b8bdaef5b452452c199b0134b5c8464c7b" alt="Image" width="33%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ac7a8aa3-fa7a-412b-95f0-757900c120f8/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_638_546_1038_858.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A58Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb43060b2c346ce3fa05d478e1fc40fcbda81436a9b9c6b6d6ededf410c4b0c33" alt="Image" width="33%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ac7a8aa3-fa7a-412b-95f0-757900c120f8/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_233_872_609_1219.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A59Z%2F-1%2F%2F91d263573d4a2db403c0a72799486040dd8d65947923bec28215be92a9fee3ae" alt="Image" width="31%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ac7a8aa3-fa7a-412b-95f0-757900c120f8/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_671_878_1025_1212.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A02%3A59Z%2F-1%2F%2F85ac25c906e2511ecbb6ba6520176134e06b02f70bc0b8790d8a9822bf6856a2" alt="Image" width="29%" /></div>


I think the most unpleasant season is winter. It's too cold to go out. I'm a person who is afraid of the cold. If it is very cold, I'll catch a cold which is really boring. I just keep coughing and sneezing all day.

Yes, I also dislike winter. In addition to the cold, it's not convenient to take part in physical sports because everybody would be dressed in thick clothes. Besides, if it's snowing, the road surface will be very slippery, and I will have to try to keep balance while walking on it. I often tumble in the winter, and I hate it!

Oh, maybe what you said just now is reasonable in a sense. It's true that winter brings us some inconveniences, but there is so much joy in this season. We can go out to take part in some outdoor sports with our families or good friends, such as skiing, skating, making snowmen, and having snowball fights. You won't be cold

when you do so. You won't catch a cold, because your physical health can be improved by taking part in such activities. And I love snow which is the most beautiful thing in the world, I think. It's so white and pure.

Yes, I agree with you. And I think the most unpleasant season is summer. It's too hot. Everybody is sweating day and night. We have to take a bath and wash clothes every day. It's too tiresome. We have many other things to do such as study. And I can't fall asleep at night because of the heat. And every summer, we have to have final examinations which is the most unpleasant thing to us students.

But in summer, we can have a long vacation after the unpleasant final examinations. Almost all our students are longing for the long vacation. So I don't think summer is the most unpleasant season.

B Then can you tell us your opinion about the most unpleasant season?

A Oh, no problem. I think spring is the most unpleasant season.

My god! Why do you think so? I think spring is the loveliest season of the year. It's a season full of hope, isn't it?

A Yes, of course. In spring, the world is full of life. But meanwhile, bacillus also come to prosper, and the weather is fluky. These two things make people fall sick easily. So in spring, many people will catch a cold. At the gate of the hospital, there are always many people standing in a line waiting for their turns to see a doctor. I often fall sick in spring, so I think spring is the most unpleasant season. As for me, I like winter most.

I just think the reason why you often fall sick is not spring, but your poor physical condition. What you should do is to take part in sports such as jogging to better your physical health. If you are strong, I think you won't look on spring as the most unpleasant season. In fact, with the green color in my eyes, I think it's the loveliest season.

Note:

sneeze: 打喷嚏 fluky: 变化无常的

inconvenience: 不便之处 fall sick: 生病

bacilli: 细菌，杆菌

技巧点拨：

在上面的讨论中,每个人都积极地参加讨论,有问有答,气氛非常好,是考生值得学习的地方。

☑ Why did you say you like winter most?

I like taking part in outdoor sports, and my favorite sport is skiing. Only in winter can I go out to ski. Although it's cold, I can feel the joy of fighting against nature, and I like this feeling. Another reason why I like winter most is that winter is the season that draws near the New Year. When winter slowly passes, a new

year will be born. What's more, in the Spring Festival, all the families have a wonderful family reunion.

Note:

family reunion: 合家团聚

Do you often listen to weather forecasts? Are they accurate?

Yes, I often listen to weather forecasts. I can get information about the weather from TV set, radio and Internet. Having known the weather for the next day, I can prepare clothes or umbrella in advance. Generally speaking, the weather forecasts are accurate. After all, the weather is forecasted by some of the most advanced technology in the world such as computer and satellite.

技巧点拨：

本题有两问，是考生需要注意的地方。回答预报是否准确的问题时，考生可以通过自身的体会，以及天气预报是用先进的科技手段得到的这两个角度来做肯定的回答。

☑ What sort of weather do you prefer? (Why?)

I prefer sunny and warm days. If it's sunny, I am very happy walking in the sunshine. May and September are my favorite months of the year. During these two months, the sunshine and temperature are both agreeable. The trees and grass are green and nature is beautiful. After supper, I like to go out and have a walk on campus. With the breeze brushing against my face gently, I feel very comfortable and relaxed. I don't need to be dressed in thick clothes because of the cold, or sweat bullets because of the heat. I can easily take part in outdoor sports such as football and jogging.

##### 技巧点拨：

好天气不仅要晴朗，更要温暖，关键在于考生如何来说明原因。上述答案中，考生既谈到了天气宜人的一面，也谈到了在这样的天气里易于从事体育锻炼的好处，从两个角度说明了原因。

What kind of climate do you think is good for elderly people? (Why?)

Certainly the sunny and dry climate. Firstly, sunny days can put elderly people in a good mood. On sunny days, elderly people can take part in outdoor sports such as jogging, which is a very good form of slow-paced sport. Elderly people can also have a walk in the parks, to chat or dance with other elderly people. It's enjoyable for the elderly people to take part in such sports. Otherwise, they would have to stay at home alone. Besides, dry climate is a must for elderly people's health. Most of elderly people have arthritis, and they will feel pain in humid weather.

Note:

arthritis: 关节炎 humid: 潮湿的

技巧点拨：

了解老人的生活习惯以及他们普遍的身体状况的话，这道问题就不难回答。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//9ad89f60-f237-4a28-84f6-c0e3a9ef4f03/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_119_451_618_795.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A09Z%2F-1%2F%2F87b947411ab9f3ca6882a6d307c35d7987f0b68d69e02bea8073c9a4c3759708" alt="Image" width="41%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">春暖花开</div> </div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//9ad89f60-f237-4a28-84f6-c0e3a9ef4f03/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_624_449_995_766.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A10Z%2F-1%2F%2F052308078abec42b868b7cefe2fdc7ce33b594842bd174505fc4544fa4643f88" alt="Image" width="31%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">冰雪解冻 河水泛滥</div> </div>


My pictures are about spring. We can feel the coming of spring by these two pictures. In the first picture, the trees are burgeoning, so the light green color is spreading endlessly before my eyes. The warm breeze is gently brushing people's faces. Flowers are blooming. The world is full of life. Poets describe spring as a season full of hope. In the second picture, ice and snow are melting. We can see water in river flowing happily in the sunshine of spring. However, too much ice and snow can bring us flood sometimes. Spring is the beginning of a year. As a good beginning is half done, we should especially value spring and try to make full use of it. Sow seeds in spring and you will get a good harvest in autumn. Then you will see how important spring is and how lovely it is!

##### Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

☑ What was the weather like during the May Day holidays?

A Oh, the weather during the May Day holidays is always very fine. May is the end of spring and beginning of summer. In the May Day holidays, the weather isn't fluky any more as that of spring and not hot as that of summer. Temperature is agreeable; every tree is green; flowers are blooming. The scenery is really very beautiful. So, in the May Day week-long holidays, many families like to go traveling.

##### 技巧点拨：

题目要求说的是五一假期期间的天气。很显然，肯定的回答是最适宜的。因为这个时期属于春末夏初，而且很多人在假期出去旅行。所有这些，在描述过程中都可以提到。

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//9ad89f60-f237-4a28-84f6-c0e3a9ef4f03/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_169_287_865_644.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A12Z%2F-1%2F%2Ff5ef761784903c71f9643919345ece3f79d760435cad9394b24e27fa7463113a" alt="Image" width="58%" /></div>


Autumn is a season of harvest. In the first picture, on the trees are plenty of fruits and the golden paddy field is endless before my eyes. A tractor is reaping the paddies. I can imagine the peasants' smiles on their faces. The weather in autumn is very good. It's sunny and the temperature is agreeable. Many people go out to have a travel in this season. In the second picture, a girl with a traveling bag on her back is climbing a mountain. The scenery in this picture is very beautiful. Certainly, in this season, leaves begin to fall and flowers begin to wither away. This is why some people say that autumn is a season of sadness and loneliness, but I don't think so. I like autumn because it's a season of harvest. There will be no beginning without ending.

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

A Which season is best for tourism?

Obviously autumn is the best season for tourism. In autumn, it's sunny and warm. Rain will be less often than that of spring and summer. Temperatures will be lower than that of summer and higher than that of winter. You can climb mountains without worrying about the slippery roads in raining or snowing weather. Temperature is steadier than that of spring, so you need not take several kinds of clothes in your traveling bag. What's more, in autumn there is a week long holiday, National Day holiday. It's really a good chance to travel.

##### Note:

steady: 稳定的

技巧点拨：

在根据手中卡片的提示来描述一年四季的时候，考生不仅要详细地将卡片上的各项内容都提到，另外还需要考生用比较优美的语言进行描述。

#### 口试全真预测试题（八）及答案

##### 【主题】 $ \underline{\text{Environment protection}} $

##### Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Are you satisfied with the environment you are living in?

A I'm dissatisfied with the environment I'm living in now. I'm a university student in Beijing. Actually I think the environment on campus is very good, because there are many trees and plenty of grass on campus. For example, at dusk, we can see many birds flying over the campus. It's very beautiful. What makes me dissatisfied is the environment of Beijing. Some of Beijing's rivers are very dirty and smell feculent. Beijing also has sensible sandstorms. This environment problem is getting more and more serious. As the suffusing dust blocks out the sky, enshrouding the city, the storm brings much inconvenience and trouble to people's daily life and work, and even claims lives in regions where the problem is more serious.

Note:

at dusk: 黄昏时刻

suffuse: 充满

feculent: 臭的

enshroud: 掩盖

##### 技巧点拨：

此题可以说是从两个角度来回答的，并且举了沙尘暴这个非常典型的例子，描述得非常细致。

Has your university done anything to protect the campus environment? (If yes, what has it done? /If not, why not?)

Yes, my university attaches great importance to the campus environment. There are many lawns and trees in my university. Workers often mow the lawns and clean the streets on campus. So, the campus's always kept tidy. There is a very beautiful lake on campus. After lunch, students and teachers always go there to have a walk. Three years ago, the water of this lake was very dirty, and teachers and students often complained about it. Then, my university mobilized all the students and teachers to clean this lake. Firstly, they drained all the water. Secondly, they scooped out the garbage that sunk to the bottom of the lake. Finally, they pumped clean water into the lake. Now the lake has restored its beauty. This is one example of what is being done to pro-

tect the campus environment, from which we can see all teachers and students are contributing to the campus environment at protection.

Note:

lawn: 草坪 drain: 排放

scoop: 铲

##### 技巧点拨：

有关校园环境的保护，每个学校都做出很多的努力。同时不仅是学校，学生对此也应有所贡献。在列举学校和学生为此所做的努力时，不妨详细展开其中某件事情。

Why is environmental protection important?

To know the importance of environmental protection, you should know the damage done by pollution. Pollution is harmful to human beings. Polluted water causes many kinds of diseases. And sometimes these diseases kill people's lives. Dirty air or garbage causes stinging eyes and running noses. Pollutants are destructive. They eat away the stone and cause iron to rust. Environmental pollution is more serious in some areas.

Note:

stinging: 刺激的

##### 技巧点拨：

实际上，上面所给出的参考答案是从反面，即环境污染所带来的不利影响，来论证环境保护的重要性，论证得非常有力。所以在口语考试中，如果从正面谈，不是很好谈的话，考生可以考虑从侧面或者是反面来进行论证。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//66073a52-1a93-4153-a7c9-76c9ac7a1830/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_126_1110_160_1141.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A07Z%2F-1%2F%2F54239e1658722bdb4a2e95a193136d31a77371cbef50d8d0b826c2c62b8a9f96" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


E Is environmental pollution a local or global problem? (Please explain)

In my opinion, environmental pollution is both a local and global problem. Environmental pollution does great damage to people's health and drives a growing number of people into poverty. So in many nations, environmental degradation is now recognized as a key barrier to governments' ability to meet basic needs and sustain living standard. At the global level, almost all of the indicators are negative. Each year now, the level of greenhouse gases in the atmosphere reaches a new high, and the ozone layer grows thinner. Thousands of plant and animal species no longer exist. The world environmental protection has, in a sense, been under way for more than two decades. Grass-roots environmental organizations proliferated in these years. Environmental pollution is a serious problem that needs to be solved by all human beings both on a local and global level.

Note:

degradation: 降级，恶化

ozone layer: 臭氧层

species: 种类

### Which do you think is more important, environmental protection or economic development?

Some people think that the most urgent task is to develop economy on a large scale because many people, especially in rural areas, are living in poverty. They believe only by further balancing and developing our economy can we get rid of poverty and gain overall prosperity. On the other hand, many people insist that environmental protection should be top priority. We have only one mother earth. If our ecological environment is heavily polluted or ruined, we could not survive on this planet. In my opinion, both economy and environment protection are urgent tasks. Without economic development, we would remain backward and possibly be conquered or bullied by other powerful countries. On the other hand without environmental protection, we would lose our home. Therefore, the best solution is to develop environmental-protection-oriented industries and agriculture, killing two birds with one stone.

##### Note:

rural: 农村的

kill two birds with one stone:一石二鸟

技巧点拨：

考生在回答二选一的问题中，可以从另外一个角度来表达自己独特的观点，只要言之有理就行。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//66073a52-1a93-4153-a7c9-76c9ac7a1830/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_386_1132_832_1449.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A09Z%2F-1%2F%2Fdb2dbdcb2d9951570c6e01f542e743582702413bf3b3e891afc8d44893a69c04" alt="Image" width="37%" /></div>


I think water pollution is most harmful to our health. Our body is mostly made of water. So, polluted water is most harmful to our health. Drinking unclean water will make you sick and sometimes kill you. To avoid this, nowadays most people buy pure water or mineral water. Many families have their own purified water dispenser.

B In fact, the tap water used in our families has been processed by the tap water factory. It is of no harm to our health. We don't use or drink the water in rivers, which is dirty and poisonous. It's difficult to process polluted water, but polluted water doesn't do damage to our health directly. Maybe water pollution is most harmful to the species living in it.

Yes, I think so. Water pollution is not the most harmful to care. As far as I am concerned, air pollution is most harmful. As everybody knows, polluted air is poisonous and it does damage to our health. If the air contains impurities, they may be absorbed by our bodies and make us ill. We need clean air, but unfortunately, air pollution is generally present, especially in cities. But it is impossible to process the air before we breathe it, as how the tap water is. The only thing we can do is decrease the amount of pollutants given off by the motor vehicles and factories.

B I'm afraid I can't agree with you. Water pollution and air pollution are both harmful to our physical health, but noise pollution is harmful to our psychological health. From my point of view, psychological health is more important than physical health.

But how can you say so? I say psychological health and physical health are both important to us. Can you give us your reasons?

The reason is very simple. We human beings are different from other creatures. If there is something wrong with our minds, physical health is meaningless. We can't work and study; we can't lead our lives happily. I'd rather choose death.

Maybe what you said is reasonable to some extent. But physical health is the foundation of psychological health. If you feel uncomfortable physically, it's obvious that you won't be comfortable psychologically.

I couldn't agree more. But I think air pollution is more harmful than water pollution. If the air pollution existed in only one place, it would influence the air of other places. The air we breathe is the same to some extent. Nowadays, no area in the world is completely free of air pollution. We must take measures to control it. So, I think air pollution is most harmful to our health.

Note:

water fountain: 饮水器

tap water: 自来水

psychological: 精神上的

What can we do to make people more environmentally conscious?

Firstly, mass media is responsible for making people more environmentally conscious. For example, commonwealth advertisement should be often broadcast on television, in newspapers, and on the radio. Secondly, the government is also

responsible for this. Government should invest more money into activities which will make people more environmentally conscious. Finally, every citizen is responsible too. In our daily lives, we should be conscious of protecting the environment. If people around us are doing things harmful to the environment, we should stop them.

##### Note:

commonwealth advertisement: 公益广告

技巧点拨：

题目问的是人们应该怎样去提高公民的环保意识。上述答案回答得比较巧妙，它是从不同的人、不同的角色应该怎样做的角度来回答的，层次非常清楚。

A Where do you find more pollution, in the cities or in the countryside? Why do you think so?

Certainly there is more pollution in the cities. Our cities have many factories to make food products, clothing, and many other things we need. Every day these factories pour millions upon millions of tons of smoke and soot into the air. Power plants that burn coal add greatly to air pollution. Our cities also have many motor vehicles, which give off numerous poisonous gases to the atmosphere. Millions of people live in big cities, producing a great deal of garbage.

##### 技巧点拨：

在回答二选一的问题时，最好选择比较好回答的那种。

##### What can the city government do to promote environmental protection?

A Firstly, the city government should invest more money in constructing facilities of environmental protection, such as garbage processing factories. Secondly, the city government should invest money to make people more environmentally conscious. It can sponsor discussion on this topic, and encourage companies to make common-wealth advertisements. Finally, the most important thing the city government should do is strengthen the supervision of environmental pollution.

##### 技巧点拨：

政府应该为保护环境所做的工作有很多，但如果考生举

不出很多例子的话，可以针对其中某个方面多举几个例子。

##### Do you think the environment today is cleaner than before?

Yes, of course. But I can only speak for Beijing. Beijing is much cleaner than before. Streets are tidier; rivers are cleaner; air is purer. The government and citizens of Beijing have done much to clean the environment. Three years ago, all taxis were replaced by Xiali or Fukang, which give off less poisonous gases than that of minibuses. People are also more environmentally conscious.

Note:

minibus: 面包车

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//79c28044-45b0-413d-a1eb-56ce4a4feda5/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_453_441_764_660.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A06Z%2F-1%2F%2Fa3a9114761b6fa6f14d1ef7c7d38d77aafcc555e85519a18b93b0d9436a998cd" alt="Image" width="26%" /></div>


In my picture, we can see one kind of pollution: air pollution. Clean air is important to good health. If the air contains impurities, our bodies may absorb it and become ill. We need clean air, but unfortunately, air pollution is generally present, especially in cities. Our cities have many factories that make food products, clothing, and many other things we need. Every day these factories pour millions upon millions of tons of smoke and soot into the air. Power plants that burn coal add greatly to air pollution. Every day, millions of motor vehicles such as buses and private cars are taking in clean air and replacing it with poisonous gases. No area in the world is completely free of air pollution. We must take measures to control it.

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

B What can we do as individuals to protect the environment?

We should show great consideration upon environmental protection in every aspect even if it seems very little. There are many things we can do to protect the environment in our daily lives. We should not waste water. Before we chuck garbage, we should divide the garbage into recyclable garbage, plastic, and so on. We should not throw garbage here and there.

##### 技巧点拨：

在回答这个问题的时候，可以举出很多平时为保护环境

应该做的事情。

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

Once you hear the word “pollution”, you may think about air pollution, water pollution, and “white” pollution. But there is another kind of pollution that is always

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//79c28044-45b0-413d-a1eb-56ce4a4feda5/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_366_170_779_542.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A08Z%2F-1%2F%2F73209ca03ee1cda46beff3c851c841d96d42ef8bff27703f889d5b17093fbcb1" alt="Image" width="34%" /></div>


neglected by people. That's noise pollution. There is so much noise in our daily lives. In this picture, a worker is digging the road surface by some kind of machine; the cars are running in the street. They make so much noise that the residents who live beside the street can't bear it any longer. Many people are looking out of their windows to see what's going on. They all want to stop the annoying noise. Living in big cities, we have to put up with so much noise. In fact, noise does great damage to the health of people. It should be regulated by our government.

Note:

waterspout: 排水口 diarrhea: 腹泻

workshop: 厂房 impurities: 杂质

sewage: 污水 annoying: 让人恼怒的

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

What do you think should be done to protect the environment?

Now, more and more people are realizing the importance of clean air. Schools are now teaching about pollution problems. Industries are beginning to install equipment to clean up their smoke. Scientists and inventors are trying to develop cleaner engines for cars and trains. Someday, we may drive electric cars. A new kind of paper that can dissolve in water and not need to be burned will be invented. A new kind of glass bottle that will melt in sunlight and disappear is being developed in some countries. Everybody is trying their best to protect the environment.

技巧点拨：

在描述图片的时候，一定要仔细观察图片，将图片中的各项内容都说到。若内容稍嫌不足，可以适当展开一下。

#### 口试全真预测试题(九)及答案

##### 【主题】 $ \underline{\text{Holiday and Festival}} $

Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

E What do you usually do during the holiday?

A Different people spend their holidays in different ways. As for me, during the holidays I most like to travel. You know, nowadays in China almost all middle school students have no time to see the outside world, they have to bury themselves to heavy assignments for the College Entrance Examination. There is a saying that reading volumes of books and trudging long distances, we learn much knowledge from books. But in modern society much more information comes from outside of class. Traveling not only increases my field of vision but also builds up my body, so I choose it.

Note:

as for: 至于

exam of entrance to college: 高考，大学入学考试

build up：增进，增大，增强

技巧点拨：

考生大约有一分钟左右的时间回答问题，要说上八九句左右。所以不能简单地回答一两句就结束。这部分没有准备时间，考生在回答问题时要一边考虑一边组织句子，所以平常要积累一些常用的句式，以便考试时争取时间。

E What do you think of western festivals, for example, Mother's Day, Valentine's Day, Christmas Day, etc.?

In recent years, many western festivals have begun to catch on in China. Among these festivals Christmas' Day, Mother's Day, Valentine's Day are the most popular. Although they are not traditionally celebrated in China, more and more Chinese are now observing these romantic Western occasions. These festivals help decorate our life and make it rich and colorful. But some Chinese youths pay less and less attention to some traditional Chinese holidays. For example, the Pure Brightness Festival, a time for worshipping at ancestral graves, is almost forgotten by youths. Fortunately, more and more Chinese begin to worry about this phenomenon.

Note:

catch on: 流行

Valentine's Day：情人节（西方节日，2月14日）

rich and colorful: 多姿多彩

Pure Brightness Festival: 清明节(中国传统节日)

技巧点拨：

任何考试都离不开一定的文化环境，所以考生平常就要注意积累关于不同国家的风俗习惯之间的差异方面的知识。一方面可以更好地理解语言的确切含义，另一方面可以在需要时用起来得心应手。

# Which festival do you prefer to celebrate, western or eastern?

When I was a child, I liked New Year most. I would be excited one month before that day was coming. In a child's eyes, New Year means finery and no homework. When I grow up, I begin to understand my parents' hard work. So when I leave home to begin my college life, I prefer Mother's Day. It makes me study harder. I phone my mother in that day and say I love her. Although I have no girlfriend now, I know most girls prefer their boyfriends to present a bunch of flowers on Valentine's Day. Western and eastern festivals are both for the sake of making our lives rich and colorful, so I like them all.

##### Note:

finery：华丽的服饰，漂亮，装饰，服饰，鲜艳服装

技巧点拨：

回答问题时要注意展开思路,可以对考官的问题加以深化,然后按照时间顺序及事情发展的过程回答问题,这样给人一种逻辑清晰的印象。

E What is your most favorite festival and why?

My most favorite festival is the Mid-autumn Festival. During the Mid-autumn Festival, there is a scene of great joy everywhere. On the Mid-autumn Festival night the whole family gets together and has a wonderful supper. During the Mid-autumn festival, it seems that everyone is in a happy mood, no matter whether you are rich or poor, young or old. There is a friendly and happy atmosphere everywhere. All family members enjoy the same gladness, so I like Mid-autumn Festival most.

##### 技巧点拨：

此题重在突出为什么最喜欢这个节日，要求答出这个节日的与众不同之处。这种问题没有“标准”答案，考生自己无论回答哪种，都要做到“自圆其说”。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//dffa2720-b15d-4ebc-a69b-3bac55a5580c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_144_173_179_203.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A06Z%2F-1%2F%2F8852071c09e6cf06d2b8ae25b25479040861fe6d2b6e25e1c03121b93f3ecbcb" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


E Where would you like to spend your holidays, in the countryside or in the big city?
I would like to spend my holiday in the countryside, partly because I live in a big city and I find it very difficult to put up with such problems in cities as pollution and traffic jams. I want to taste a new way of life. In the countryside, there is always something new, something fresh to see. Taking a holiday in the countryside, you'll feel relaxed both in mind and body. Besides, it's marvelous to get close to nature. I can go fishing after lunch every day, and also raise dogs without worrying about disturbing the neighbors.

##### Note:

jam：堵塞，轧住，拥挤

marvelous：引起惊异的，不可思议的，非凡的，惊喜的

技巧点拨：

这种问题自由度较大,可以根据自己的生活经验来谈,也可以发挥自己的想像力,但还是要从不同的角度对比论述,这样才能使答案较为丰满。

During these years some people call Labor Day and National Day golden-week, what is your opinion about this?

Previously people only knew they could relax in holiday. With the society developing, many people have come to realize that holidays can stimulate the growth of the economy. If you walk down the street just before holidays, you will see lots of people spending their money on all sorts of items, such as computers and expensive clothes. With longer holidays people in China will spend their money on traveling, shopping and so on instead of saving. The circulation of currency is promoted in this way and some businesses such as tourism are booming. On the other hand, since people have more time to relax, their work efficiency has been greatly enhanced. Consequently, the growth of the economy has reached a high level. So the Labor Day and Nation Day are called golden-week.

Note:

circulation of currency: 货币流通

golden-week：黄金周

技巧点拨：

有些话题会涉及到经济，所以考生平时要多注意思考，在现实社会中，经济是最重要、最敏感的一个方面，日常生活中的很多事情都会或多或少地打上经济的烙印。有些看似无关的事情，在深层次里存在着必然的联系，所以考生要善于通过表面现象分析问题的实质。

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Are there disadvantages of celebrating a holiday or not?</div> </div>


day can make you sick without your notice. People eat and drink too much, which is unhealthy, and many people get sick. Secondly, it brings pollution to environment; we can see more and more scenery and historic sites are destroyed by tourists. Thirdly, it's a waste of money. Usually during holidays people will spend a large sum of money on presents, food, clothes, traveling and camping. All these produce a lot of waste and its plastic bags then cause pollution.

##### 技巧点拨：

任何事情都有正反两面，机遇总是留给勤于思考的人。假日经济给人们带来身心放松的同时，也会给社会带来负面影响，如铺张浪费、环境污染等等。所以我们在谈到享受假日的惬意时，更不要忘记提及加强环保意识。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

In my opinion, my favorite way of spending the holiday is to be with family. After all, Chinese people are very traditional and respect ancient traditions.

I don't agree with you. With the development of civilization, we need more friends and have to get more information from outside, so we should add a new dimension to the way of spending a holiday.

As for me, the best way of spending a holiday is shopping, because there is always a big sale during the holidays. We have no time to go shopping on working days. Shopping caters for women's pleasure as well.

I prefer to surf the net during holidays. With the development of society, many good friends are far away from us. I can't afford the phone bill, so we usually keep in touch with them through Internet. It makes us nearer.

That's right. The internet is popular among young people. It's hard to image if we depart from the web.

I think we should have a mild attitude towards holidays. Our main aim is to relax, so whatever ways we choose to get through our holidays, we shouldn't expend too much energy, because after holiday we have many things to do.

技巧点拨：

这段讨论要紧扣要求展开，讨论既不是一个个轮流发言，也不是一个人演讲，要积极参与，但又不能过于以自我为中心，要体现“讨论”这一方式。以上这几个考生很好地做到了这一点，参加讨论的同学各抒己见，观点鲜明，讨论得非常成功。

### Nowadays China traditional festivals are struck by western ones, and what's your opinion about this phenomenon?

I think this phenomenon has something to do with the open-door policy and the popularity of English language in our country. Since the beginning of the 90's, English seems to play a more and more important role in our society. With a good knowledge of English, you can easily find a job with a fairly high salary. And it's a passport to most people, especially youths who are willing to go abroad to pursue further studies. Without English, you can't even use the computer, the most widely used apparatus in modern society, and have still less chance to travel on the internet. Most advanced technology is introduced into the country originally in English. What's more, many western countries are more powerful than China, so shouldn't we first learn as much about it as possible before we could overtake it in economy?

##### Note:

pursue further studies: 继续深造

travel on the internet: 网上冲浪

##### 技巧点拨：

这是口语考试的最后一部分，如果考生前面发挥得不够理想，这是一次弥补机会，可以尽量回答得充分些、积极些。如果前面发挥得还不错，要争取这个部分锦上添花，善始善终。

中国的改革开放使外语学习显得非常重要，而语言和文化密不可分。作为文化的载体，语言学习离不开相应的文化的学习。节日是文化的重要组成部分，所以透过西方的节日，可以看到西方文化的方方面面。

A Why do you like western festivals?

I like these western festivals. Just now the student above talked so much about learning the language and culture. No doubt it is reasonable. But I join the growing numbers who celebrate these festivals, not only because it's foreign. In my opinion, these festivals just express more concern, more caring about individual feelings than their Chinese counterparts do. For example, for Christmas, we prepare and send gifts to friends or the older, and always give each other a surprise. On Valentine's Day, not only young people, but people of various ages send roses, chocolates to the person he or she loves. It just expresses their bless for the person they love. Isn't it an enjoyable thing?

##### Note:

counterparts：与之相对应的中国的节日

技巧点拨：

本题从节日本身的文化内涵和特质出发，指出西方节日更注重个人感情的表达，而由此喜欢西方节日。

Just now the student said that people celebrate the western festivals because they want to learn their culture. But it's not always the case for every person. Some young people just think lightly of our own culture. They hold the view that foreign countries are more developed than us and we should learn from them. Certainly it's true, but what we should learn is science and more advanced technology, and also, not just paying attention to their life style. Additionally, the advertising of the businessmen enhanced this phenomenon, a few days before the festival especially the Christmas, we can see the advertisements and decorations at the gate of restaurants, hotels, supermarkets and even little stores. In this way they have created the atmosphere of celebrating these western festivals.

##### Note:

to think lightly of: 轻看

decoration：装饰，装饰品

技巧点拨：

这位同学通过对这种现象深入剖析之后，指出造成这种现象的原因有两个：一是这些年轻人认为国外的东西比国内的好，轻视自己的传统文化；一是在利益的驱动下，商家推波助澜的结果。这些都造成了时下西方节日更受欢迎的局面。

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//dffa2720-b15d-4ebc-a69b-3bac55a5580c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_436_1045_745_1387.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A10Z%2F-1%2F%2Fde4e3751ef5401ea77ae3392808cbe63ea94764d13d39b5bb61ad53785952afa" alt="Image" width="25%" /></div>


The picture shows us a prospect of family reunion. The whole family is getting together and having a wonderful supper. In holidays they choose to stay home to enjoy and relax with the family. Each member of the family appears to be happy and they are toasting to each other's health. In China family reunion is a traditional way of observing holidays.

##### Note:

toast to each other's health: 相互干杯祝福对方身体健康

Thank you. Now, candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

Do you think we should spend our own festivals or western ones?

A I'm a more traditional person. I've also noticed the change and my attitude toward this phenomenon is just live-and-let-live. People can have their own way of life. If they want to celebrate the western festivals, then just let them do so. But for myself, I prefer celebrating our traditional ones. Chinese are more reserved. And being reserved is not a fault in our character. We just show our love in our own way. It may not be spoken out, but one can feel it. And usually it's the deepest, and the strongest love. Under the more reserved appearance, there is often a more colorful and richer inner world. So it's still more important to celebrate our own festivals.

Note:

live-and-let-live：宽以待人的态度

reserved: 含蓄的

技巧点拨：

这位同学认为，过哪个国家的节日是自己的选择，但是通过对中国文化中的含蓄一面做深入剖析之后，态度鲜明地倡导我们应该过自己的节日。我们炎黄子孙虽然表面上比较含蓄，但内心的感情更为深沉、更为丰富。这位同学论述得很有力度。

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//9023e975-5734-4c4b-9c26-b9b7b8bf8f5c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_398_1018_588_1208.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A19Z%2F-1%2F%2Fea2697c0bd39bf105e736431e457f2ff65e0256e38f0db29f409f66c3c295a1a" alt="Image" width="15%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//9023e975-5734-4c4b-9c26-b9b7b8bf8f5c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_646_1016_838_1207.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A20Z%2F-1%2F%2F59202a33e3a8996114b76e87fe3922556387a5d9142515aa8fd0fdb2a1fdfdee" alt="Image" width="16%" /></div>


In the left of pictures, groups of people, mostly women, are shopping in the stories. They are purchasing all kinds of clothes. In holidays people usually stroll along the streets. There are usually many sales for them to get a good bargain. In the right picture, a boy indulges himself in the net. Surfing the net seems to have brought great satisfaction to him. Indeed surfing the net is convenient, but we should be aware of the problems as well. The web can be dangerous because it can be addictive and violent sites are easily accessible and can pollute young minds. Additionally, it influences your working and studying play if you spend too much time on it. So, people should be careful not to be misled.

Note:

strolling along the street: 逛街，溜达

addictive: 上瘾的

Thank you. Now, candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

What should we do to encounter these phenomenon?

In my opinion, at present, we have two urgent tasks at hand. One is to develop our economy quickly, it can help us win back the youth. The other is to publicize and give more attention to our own culture. We can create a kind of atmosphere with the help of government, and our mass media. We should learn from the foreigners but we should have our own understanding to these festivals. For example, we could have a candle supper with our lovers without rose or chocolate, we could express the same feeling by our own ways.

Note:

publicize: 宣扬

mass media：大众传媒

candle supper: 烛光晚餐

技巧点拨：

在这部分，首先要注意听清考官的要求，严格按照指定的要求去描述；其次还要注意时间，一般在这部分的看图叙述中都要求进行比较描述，所以要留一部分时间进行比较描述。只要掌握好了这两点，看图描述的题目还是不难的。

#### 口试全真预测试题（十）及答案

##### 【主题】 Communication

##### Part 1 (2 minutes)

First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves.

Which way of communication do you commonly use to contact your friends?

Generally speaking, I have different ways of communication depending on different things and time. At the beginning of my college I kept up correspondence with my friends, especially with those who didn't live in the same cities or provinces. For friends that stayed in the same city, I usually phoned them. Sometimes I wrote to them. You know, the cost of long distance calls was so high that I couldn't afford it. Three years ago, I had more ways to choose. Now I prefer to communicate with them on the computer because we all have our own computers. Of course I'd like to use the most efficient way to communicate with them no matter how far away they live if there is something important or urgent to tell them.

Note:

generally speaking: 一般来说

keep up correspondence with: 通过信件(联系)

技巧点拨：

这个问题是针对同学们日常生活中的一个生活习惯的提问，不管实际采用几种方式，在回答问题时还是最好多说几种，并简要加以说明，以使回答更加充实、有条理，千万不能简单地一句话回答了事，毕竟考试和日常生活是有一定区别的。

Can you list some means of communication?

A Ok, I think the most common means of communication is the telephone. But we have other traditional ways of communication, for example, write letters, send telegrams and talk face to face. It is surveyed that nowadays most households in the city owe at least one telephone, through which people can easily talk to each other. Letters was used most often by those who live in the countryside where phones are not available yet. But nowadays phones are very popular in the countryside that even people over there write less letters to each other. Recently many people have found a new way: sending short letters by mobile-phone. With the popularity of computers, more and more people now start to send e-mails to each other, but not everyone can afford to buy a computer. College students and employees in the city often use this way.

Note:

It is surveyed that: 据调查

send a short letter by mobile-phone: 通过手机发短信

##### 技巧点拨：

这个话题要求考生对各种通讯方式都比较熟悉，作为在校大学生来说，有较多的机会尝试各种通讯方式，所以这个问题比较好回答。从前面我们回答的问题可以看出，无论回答什么类型的提问，答案的组织总有一个内在的逻辑顺序，比如时间顺序，因果顺序，主次顺序等等。英语语言习惯比较重视内在的逻辑性和具体性，所以考生若要说出符合英语语言习惯的句子来，必须在这两方面下工夫。

As a special group, how do college school students contact their family members and friends?

I don't know how to communicate with my family members because I have nothing to say and phone them only one a year to say please remit me some money (laugh). As far as my classmates are concerned, most students send e-mails to communicate. This can be proven by their opening e-mail boxes several times every day. But on some occasions, such as one week before New Year's Day, there will be many letters flying to our class just like snowflake from all over the nation. You can see lots of students standing in a long queue for stamps. Sometimes you can also see many students use public telephones but most of them talk on the phone to their friends who just live in the same city or in the same school.

##### Note:

remit：汇、寄（款等）；传送

just like snowflake: 如雪花般

stand in a long queue: 排长队

##### 技巧点拨：

这位同学的回答给你一种轻松愉快的感觉。考试其实也是一种平等的交流，如果考生能适当地通过插入一两句调侃的话调节一下气氛，肯定能调动大家的积极性，同时给老师留下深刻的印象。当然，这是建立在准确的表达之上的，如果说出来的话不能引起大家的共鸣，只能是画蛇添足，适得其反。所以考生要灵活运用这一技巧。

Do you think modern communication make people closer?

Firstly, modern traffic tools let people walk farther than whenever. The whole earth is increasingly becoming a village, correspondingly, modern communication make people closer. Wherever you are, if you can login web, you will be one of the member in information express way. Several hundred years ago people communicated by horse. In the 20th century, people communicate with phone, tele-

gram, which draw the space between people nearer, and at 21st century, computer integrates with communication technology, and greatly effect our life, which make us closer.

Note:

information express way: 信息高速公路

integrate with: 和……融为一体

技巧点拨：

是通讯使我们这个地球变得越来越小，人们联系越来越方便，通讯在拉近人们距离的同时又在渐渐的增大人们的空间距离。

Could you tell us what influence computer brings to communication?

Several decades ago, people communicated with phones. At that time phones transferred information by ways of stimulation signal. It's hard to deal with and transfer large amounts of stimulation signals, especially audio and video which constitute enormous signals. After the chip was invented, the velocity of dealing with information has greatly increased, stimulation signals are turned to digital signals and transferred by the web, which make people see the picture when listening to the voice. So it can be said computer, combined with communication brings a revolution to communication technology. We have come to feel its influence and this influence will continue.

##### Note:

stimulation signal: 模拟信号

digital signal: 数字信号

技巧点拨：

科技领域的新进展越来越快地影响着我们的生活，芯片的发明给通信技术带来了一场深远的革命，它带给我们的影响远非我们的想像所及，随着科技的发展我们的生活将继续发生日新月异的变化，以上这一切都是计算机给通讯带来的影响。

Which factors students now usually consider when choosing communication ways?

B Generally speaking, cost is the first factor, after all we are now students and have no income. Secondly the quality of call must also be take account of. There are many IP phones now, but among them some have poor quality, so I won't choose it although it's cheaper than common phones. Of course concrete problem concrete analysis, if encountering something urgent, you must choose a way available immediately.

##### 技巧点拨：

回答这些问题的时候，怎么想就怎么说，关键是要把自己的答案说得条理清晰，有逻辑性。

Do you think telephone communication will increasingly replace letters in the future? And why?

In my opinion, although telephone communication is quite popular in China, it won't take the place of letters. First of all, telephones are still not available in many rural or mountainous areas. The only way for them to communicate with the outside world is by means of writing and receiving letters. Second, they want to avoid long distance calls. And most important of all, people, particularly lovers, can express delicate feelings in letters. Of course, there are even more advantages to writing a letter, for example, people can keep their letters as long as they like and reread them when they get old, which can remind them of past times. And last but most important, is formal contract which must be written on paper, not by telephone. So, that's why I don't think the telephone will replace the letter.

Note:

rural and mountainous areas: 农村和山区

delicate: 微妙的

##### 技巧点拨：

不同的通讯方式有着各自的特点和各自不可替代的优势，虽然电话使人们联系更加方面快捷，但始终无法彻底取代书信的来往，这和人们的生活习惯和思维方式甚至文化传统有着不可分割的联系。也许某一天我们真的可以接受所有的联系都可以不用书信的情况。

##### Part 2 (3 minutes)

E Now, I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6ec5f531-5603-413b-97a7-277b6e52414c/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_218_1127_934_1417.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A23Z%2F-1%2F%2F40d5ad384aed36a9e45c7e2a6185df301cb1ff61b307975d9f826c4aa3ab300a" alt="Image" width="60%" /></div>


With computer's popularity on campus, sending e-mails becomes a main way of communication. You needn't buy a computer because there are computers everywhere, so I think e-mail will be the main way of communication in the future.

It's true that e-mail communication is very advanced and has many advantages. But what I'd like to point out is that it has some drawbacks; more and more rubbish e-mails are sent into your mailbox whether you like them or not.

Right, last week I have accepted over 30 rubbish emails.

I can say for sure that e-mail communication will never take the place of postal communication. It requires people to know a little computer and English, Those who don't know English can't use it. In addition, writing a letter is cheaper than sending an e-mail.

Compared with other means of communication, letters take a long time to reach people. You know, in the information age, we attach great importance to work efficiency. All is zero without efficiency.

B Yes, I quite agree with you. That's why I prefer to send messages by e-mail.

技巧点拨：

这段讨论比较激烈，参加讨论的同学各抒己见，观点鲜明，基本体现了现代科技发展给人们带来的利弊，观点比较新颖，讨论很成功。

Can you list some ways of communication before computer comes into your life?

A Ok, when I was a middle school student, I never saw a computer, just heard that there was a department named computer science and technology. At that time I usually communicated with friends and relatives through letters and sometimes I visited them if I had some thing urgent, and if we are far away, sometimes I phoned them, but that time few students had phone, so phone was not the main communication way we used. If we meet some urgent matter, telegram turned out to be the choice. In a word, before computer comes into my life, I had only a less of ways of communications.

##### 技巧点拨：

计算机运用到通信技术之前，人们的联系方式还仅仅限于书信、电报等传统的方式，只有当网络和通讯结合之后，人们的联系才真正变得丰富多彩，才会有更多可选择的方式。

Do you think traditional means of communication have any predominance?

Modern communication technology has brought us great convenience, traditional means of communication are being paid less attention to than before, but they won't be replaced by modern communication; they have their particular predominance. Firstly, the more advanced technology the tender it is. Computer can't work if the power is out, and we have to turn to traditional communication. Secondly, some manuscript is far more valuable than electron edition, especially famous people's manuscript. Thirdly, many people feel traditional communication more reliable, which relates to a nation's culture.

##### 技巧点拨：

虽然现代通讯方式极大地改变了人们的生活，但是传统的通讯依然有其自身不可替代的优势，不同的情况会采取不同的方式。如果对问题没有很深入的思考，可以先重复一下

题目，给自己争取一些时间，然后大致理一下思路，边想边说，这也是在回答那些不太好回答的问题时的一个常用策略。

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Do modern communication have any impact on people's value?</div> </div>


A Science and technology greatly changes people's living habits, and the change of living habits must bring some influence to culture, and then have impact on people's value.

##### Part 3 (7 minutes)

I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture. Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6ec5f531-5603-413b-97a7-277b6e52414c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_407_628_763_965.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A30Z%2F-1%2F%2Fa41ac34002b5844342ac681a1f442f47835cbec6d7b28ab88818537a69016c52" alt="Image" width="29%" /></div>


Although the net is so appealing and it provides us with so many chances in cyber space, face-to-face talk still has its own charm. Whatever you do, the most reliable way is to talk to him directly, several words can make a question clear. But if you phone him, probably it will cost you much time to explain it. In addition, face-to-face chat will make you feel great and happy. Of course this kind of communication has its faulty. If he is far away from you or if you are at work in your work time, you can't chat face-to-face.

##### Note:

cyber space：计算机空间，虚拟空间

Thank you. Now, Candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

More and more security problems come in modern communication, what's about your opinion?

With the development of modern communication, on one hand cheat and violence become more and more serious on network, on the other hand there is the problem of identity authentication in e-commerce. The existing of network virus and hacker is a threat to the security. It is surveyed the whole world spends at least 20 billion

dollars every year on network security. In spite of that, the crime through network is more and more serious. In my opinion on one hand it must take a whole series of measure to protect the network from all nodes, on the other hand everyone must learn some technology against virus and hacker.

##### Note:

identity authentication: 身份认证

e-commerce：电子商务

hacker：黑客

##### 技巧点拨：

网络安全是现在比较流行的话题，对信息技术比较关心的人回答这类问题可能比较容易，但是不管我们是否关心，我们的生活正在受到它的影响。只有平常多注意多学习，才能使通过网络传达信息变得更加安全。

OK. Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//2c0eb791-3781-40f2-8ca4-baea9c5baf87/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_447_764_797_1105.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A03%3A19Z%2F-1%2F%2Fd3309bdb55520dc2a9f341d2f8c9150f0861f4164f233d891ec14aa5f2457567" alt="Image" width="29%" /></div>


Although online chatting has some disadvantages, it must admit that it has also all kinds of advantages, and just these advantages make it flourishing. First, it can increase students' interest in computer studying; only when one is interested in the field can he make progress on it. Second, it provide people with a good place to talk. We can find all kinds of chatting room on net; it's open and free. Third, you can make more friends in all kinds of fields who come from all kinds of field and have different hobbies and speciality. You can learn many knowledge from them and make friends with them. Fourth, online chatting can cure people's psychological problems, in modern society people need a place to release, net is the best place.

Note:

flourishing: 繁茂的，繁荣的，欣欣向荣的

speciality: 专长，特长

Thank you. Now, candidate A,

partner a question?

Can you assume how people communicate with others in the future?

This is an interesting topic. Several years ago we had only phone or telegram but now we can call your friend. On web and we can see him at the same time. In the future communication technology will integrate with traffic tools and we can shake hands with anyone all over the world as we wish, we even communicate with plants and animals, say nothing of the creature of remote celestial body. In the future we possibly have a chip in our brain, when we think, the counterpart can do i correspondingly.

##### Note:

celestial body: 天体

##### 技巧点拨：

根据提纲进行扩充是比较容易的一种考题，可以把它当作一个作纲作文来对待，只是要注意，在口语考试中，最好多说一些短句，太长的句子不利于老师和其他考生理解，同时自己也容易出错，这些也是作文和口语的区别。
